camayam toṭaṅkumiṭattukkuk koṇṭucellum neṟi oṉṟēyākum. aṟaneṟi celluvōṉai, atu kaṭavuḷākiya cemporuḷ uṇmaiyiṉaiyuṇartti ataṉ iruppaip pulap paṭutti, ataṉ ākkamum taṉmaiyaiyum nōkkaviṭātu ataṉatu “aṟputa nilai” yiṟ celutti viṭum. inta neṟiyaik kāṇpatu aritaṉṟu; eḷitu. āṉāl, ataṉaip piṉpaṟṟi viṭātu celpavar mikavum ariyarāvar.
iṉi, viññāṉamum, tattuva ñāṉamum, camayavuṇarvum oṉṟiṉoṉṟu toṭarpuṟṟuc ciṟantu niṟpatu kāṇattakkatu. ivvuṇmaiyaik kaṇṭu vaittum, viññāṉikaḷiṟ cilar viññāṉaṅ kāṭṭum putumaiyilum aḻakilum mūḻkit tammai maṟantu kaṭavuṭ koḷkai neṟiyil karuttaic celuttārā kiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟē tattuva ñāṉikaḷiṟ cilar, ulakak kāraṇa mutaṟporuḷaic cintikkum cintaṉaiyil āḻntu tattuva ñāṉamum camayaneṟiyum oṉṟupaṭum iṭamuṇṭeṉa uṇarkiṉṟaṉar. uṇarntum, mēṉāṭṭut tattuvañāṉikaḷ palar, avai eṅkē eppaṭi oṉṟupaṭu kiṉṟaṉa eṉpataik kāṇārāy, camayattait tamatu tattuva neṟiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟākap pirittuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. koḷḷumiṭattum, pācukal mutaliyōraip pōlac camayattaik koṇṭu, tattuvañāṉa neṟiyai vilakku vōrum, tattuva ñāṉattaik koṇṭu camayattaik kaiviṭuvōrum uḷarāyiṉar. innilaiyil cilcaṉ eṉpār muṟpōntu, “uṇmaiyai ēṉ otukkavēṇṭum? tattuva neṟiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum kaṭavuḷē camaya mutaṟkaṭavuḷāvar eṉṟu terintu koḷḷavēṇṭum” eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttu kiṉṟār.
ivvāṟu tattuva ñāṉamum camaya ñāṉamum oṉṟuk koṉṟu, paṭi muṟaiyil iruppatukaṇṭē meykaṇṭa tēvar tāmaruḷiya civañāṉa pōtattil muṉ āṟu cūttiraṅ kaḷāl tattuva ñāṉamum, piṉṉāṟu cūttiraṅkaḷāl camaya ñāṉamum vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. mēṉāṭṭavar itukāṟum tattuva ñāṉattaiyum vēṟu vēṟu vaittē pēci vantaṉar; varukiṉṟaṉar; tattuva ñāṉattiṉ muṭiviṭam camaya ñāṉat toṭakkam eṉṟu kaṇṭu, ippōtu tāṉ iraṇṭaiyum oṉṟākak koḷḷum muyaṟciyaic cey kiṉṟaṉar.
nam tamiḻ nāṭṭil tirumūlar kāla mutalē iraṇṭum oṉṟāyp pirippaṟappiṇaintu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. iṭaik kālattē mutalil camaya ñāṉamum piṉṉart tattuva ñāṉamum ciṟappu nilai peṟṟaṉa. ñāṉacampantar mutaliya camayakuravar kālattil camaya ñāṉamum, meykaṇṭār mutaliya cantāṉa kuravar kālattil tattuva ñāṉamum ciṟappuṟṟu niṉṟaṉa eṉpatu varalāṟaṟintōrkku terinta ceytiyākum.
mēlē kūṟiya viññāṉam, tattuva ñāṉam, camaya ñāṉam eṉṟa mūṉṟum caiva cittāntattiṟ kūṟappaṭum pācañāṉam, pacuñāṉam, patiñāṉam eṉṟa mūṉṟaṉ kaṇṇum aṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. pacu, pāca ñāṉaṅkaḷ ōrellai yaṭaintatum, taṭaipaṭṭu niṟpatai mēṉāṭṭaṟiñar aṟintu kūṟukiṉṟaṉareṉṟu mēlē kūṟiṉām. itaṉai muṉkūṭṭiyaṟinta tamiḻccāṉṟōr “pācañāṉat tālum pārppariya paramparaṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppataik kāṇ kiṉṟōm.
11. caivacittāntamum namnāṭṭu ēṉai 
vaitīka cittāntaṅkaḷum
iṉi, nam caivacittāntam civam, uyir, ulaku eṉṟa mūṉṟaṉaiyum aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭatu eṉa muṉpē kūṟiṉōm. civattaip patiyeṉṟum, uyiraip pacu eṉṟum, ulakaip pāca meṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvatuṇṭu. patipporuḷ civamākiya cemporu ḷākum. pacuveṉpatu uyir; pācameṉṟatu malamum viṉaiyum, uyir niṟkum uṭal, karuvi, ulaku nukarccikaḷum eṉṟavaṟṟiṉ tokuti; itupaṟṟiyē civākamaṅkaḷ ivaṟṟaip “pācacālam”* eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
paṇṭai nāḷil iṅkē poruḷkaḷaippaṟṟi nam nāṭṭut tattuva ñāṉikaḷ virinta ārāycciyaic ceytirukkiṉṟaṉar. ārāycciyāl avaravar kaṇṭa karuttukkaḷuk kēṟpa vēṟupāṭu kaḷum avariṭaiyē uṇṭāyiṉa. avvāṟṟāl mīmāñcai, yōkam, vaicēṭikam, niyāyam, cāṅkiyam mutaliya palaneṟikaḷ camayatattuvap pirivukaḷāka nilavukiṉṟaṉa. mīmāñcai, pūruvamī māñcai uttaramīmāñcai eṉa iraṇṭāy pūruvam kaṉma kāṇṭam eṉavum, uttaram ñāṉakāṇṭam eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. inta uttara mīmāñcaiyai vētāntameṉa vaḻaṅkuvatu peruvaḻakku.
ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē nam tamiḻakattil, caivattōṭu muṉṉē kūṟiya cāṅkiyam, naiyāyikam, vaicēṭikam, mīmāñcakam, pauttam, ulakāyatam eṉṟa camayaṅkaḷ nilaviṉa eṉṟu maṇimēkalai kūṟukiṟatu. iccamayaṅkaḷiṟ palavum ippōtu iṟantu pōyiṉa; eṉiṉum avvavaṟṟiṉ naṟkūṟukaḷ vaḻivaḻiyāka vantuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ pauttam, ulakāyatam oḻittu oḻinta ellām vētavaḻakkoṭuppaṭṭaṉa eṉpar. nam caivattaiyum “vētap payaṉām caivam” eṉac cēkkiḻār kuṟittaruḷukiṉṟār. ikkālac camayatattuva āciriyarkaḷ, mēlē pauttam mutalākak kūṟiyavaṟṟoṭu oppa nam caivacittāntattai oru tattuva camaya mākak karutuvatillai. intiya nāṭṭut tattuva camayaṅkaḷaippaṟṟi virittum tokuttum nūleḻutum āciriyarkaḷ evarum, mēlē kūṟiyavaṟṟōṭu oṉṟākac caivacittāntattai itukāṟum eḻutāmaiyē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟu. palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷil caivacittāntam oru tattuva ñāṉa camayamāka vaittu ārāyvatē illāmal iruntu vantatu. ippōtutāṉ aṇṇāmalaip palkalaikkaḻakam ataṉaic ceyya muṉvantuḷḷatu.
in nilaiyil caiva cittāntattukkum ēṉaic cittāntaṅ kaṭkumuḷḷa uyar voppumaikaḷai nām niṉaivu kūrvatu takkatu. iṟaivaṉ, uyir, ulakiṟku mutaṟ kāraṇa mākiya māyai eṉṟa mūṉṟum aṉātinittiyap poruḷkaḷ eṉavum, iṟaivaṉākiya civam taṉ catti caṅkaṟpattāl māyaiyaik kalakki uṭal, karuvi, ulakukaḷākac ceyvatu paṭaippeṉavum, uyirkaḷ aṉātiyē malattoṭakku uṭaiyavai yeṉavum, malattiṉ maṟaippu nīṅkutaṟ poruṭṭu uṭal karuvikaḷ paṭaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉavum, uyirkaḷ pala eṉavum, pala veṉavē uyirkaḷ oṉṟiṉoṉṟu vēṟupāṭu ṭaiyaveṉavum, vēṟupāṭṭuk kēṟpap piṟappu vēṟu paṭukiṉṟaṉa veṉavum, uyirkaṭku aṟivu, viruppu, toḻil eṉṟa mūṉṟu āṟṟal uṇṭeṉavum, avaṟṟait toḻiṟ paṭutti uṇmai ñāṉam eytutaṟ poruṭṭē ulakat toṭarpu uyir kaṭkuṇṭākiṟateṉavum, iṅṅaṉam uyir kaṭku utavum vakaiyil civaparamporuḷ uyirōṭu uṭaṉāy irukkiṉṟatu eṉavum, nam caivacittāntam kūṟukiṟatu. ivaiyum ivaṟṟōṭu iyaipuṭaiya piṟavum nīṅkaḷ aṟintavai yātalāl ivaṟṟai ñāpakappaṭutti mēṟ cellukiṉṟēṉ.
mēlē kūṟiya niyāyam, vaicēṭikam, cāṅkiyam, yōkam, mīmāñcai, mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ mīmāñcai yoḻiya ēṉaiya vellām uyirkaḷ aṉātinittameṉṟum pala veṉṟum kūṟuvaṉa; ivvula kiṟku mutaṟkāraṇam pirakiruti māyaiyeṉa yōkarum, cāṅki yarum kūṟuvar; aṇuvai mutaṟkāraṇameṉa vaicēṭikarum naiyāyikarum kūṟuvar, im mutaṟkāraṇaṅkaḷaik kalakkit toḻiṟpaṭutti uṭal, karuvi, ulakamāka ākkiya vakaiyil iṟaivaṉ nimitta kāraṇaṉākiṉṟāṉ eṉṉum karuttil ivarkaḷ palarum otta karuttuṭaiyarākiṉṟaṉar. iṟaivaṉ nimitta kāraṇa māy niṉṟu uṭal karuvi ulakukaḷaip paṭaittaṟkuk kāraṇam kūṟumiṭattu, uyirkaḷ tam tūya nilaiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅkip pirakiruti yōṭu toṭar puṟṟuk kaṭṭuṟṟaṉa veṉavum, ak kaṭṭiṉiṉṟum nīṅki vīṭupeṟu taṟkē ippaṭaippu nikaḻvatāyiṟṟeṉavum ittattuva camayaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. kēvalattil tūyavāyirunta uyirkaḷ pirakiruti toṭar puṟṟataṟku accamayaṅkaḷ ētukkāṇa māṭṭāvāyiṉa; ataṉaiyārāyntu kēvalattil malattoṭakku kāraṇa meṉak kaṇṭatu nam caivam, uyirkaḷ ceyyum viṉaip payaṉkaḷ aṟivillātaṉavātaliṉ, avaṟṟai uyirkaḷ nukarap paṇṇuvatu iṟaivaṉ ceyal eṉa naiyāyikar mutaliyōr kūṟuvar. uyirkaḷ uṇarvuṭaiyaṉa vātaliṉ avaitāmē tam viṉaip payaṉkaḷai nukaralāmē eṉṟeḻum taṭaikku vaicēṭikar āṉmā oṭukkattil ceyalaṟṟuk kiṭakkumeṉṟum, piṟar piṟavāṟum kūṟiṉārāka, malattāl maṟaippuṇṭu aṟivu, viḻaivu, ceyalkaḷ kaṭṭuṟṟu niṟkum uyir, malattāl maṟaippuṇṇum ciṟumaiyuṭai yatākaliṉ, civaparam poruḷ pōl viṉaikkuriya payaṉaik kāṇavum, kaṇṭu nukaravum iyalātātaliṉ, civaparamporuḷ uyir ceyyum viṉaip payaṉaic ceytavuyirai nukarappaṇṇuvar eṉac caivam kūṟuvatāyiṟṟu. ivvāṟu piṟacamayaṅkaḷāl viḷakka muṟāta tattuvap pakutikaḷ pala caivacittāntattil aiyantiripaṟa viḷakkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
iṟaivaṉ oruvaṉaittavira, uyirkaḷum ulaka mutaṟ kāraṇaṅ kaḷum avaṉaip pōl aṉātinittamāka iruppavai eṉpatu muṟaiyaṉṟu; aḵtu iṟaivaṉatu ellām valla taṉmaikku iḻukku eṉṟu koṇṭu, iṟaivaṉē ellāvaṟṟiṟkum mutaṟ kāraṇamum nimitta kāraṇamumāvaṉ eṉṟukūṟum tattuva camayaṅ kaḷumuṇṭu. oru kāraṇattiliruntu iraṇṭu māṟupaṭṭa iyalpu ṭaiya kāriyaṅkaḷ piṟavā eṉṟa muṟaiyum piṟavum aṭippaṭai yākak koṇṭu akkoḷkaiyai ēṟkātu vilakkum vakaiyil caivam ciṟantu niṟkiṟatu. illatiṉiṉṟum ulakum, uyirkaḷum tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṉum camayamum uṇṭu. illatu tōṉṟātu, uḷḷatē tōṉṟum eṉṉum caṟkāriya  muṟaiyāl caivam mēṉmaiyuṟukiṟatu. vaṉmai yeṉpatu paṭaippa toṉṟukkē uriyataṉṟu; toḻiṟ paṭalum payaṉukartalum ākiya ceyal valiyillātavaṟṟukku valitantu toḻiṟpaṭuttutal, payaṉ nukar vittal mutaliyavaṟṟukkuc ciṟappāka uriyatu.
ivvāṟu pala vakaiyālum, aṟivārāyccikku otta nilaiyil mēṉmaiyuṟṟut tikaḻum caivacittāntam nam nāṭṭil naṉku paraviyillai. civaṉaivaḻipaṭum caivaruḷ caivamaṟintavar mikavum arukiyēyuḷḷaṉar. caiva neṟiyai nāṭṭil parappum vakaiyil caiva aṟiñarkaḷum caiva aṟanilaiyaṅkaḷum karuttil lātu pōyiṉa eṉpatai muṉṉarē kūṟiṉēṉ.
12. caivacittānta nūlkaḷum ceytaṟkuriya paṇiyum
caivacittāntattai viḷakkutaṟku meykaṇṭa nūlkaḷum paṇṭāra cāttiraṅkaḷum tamiḻil uḷḷaṉa. avai perumpālum ceyyuḷ vaṭivil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ik kālattil tōṉṟi nilavum ceyyuṭkaḷaiyum uraivakaikaḷaiyum kāṇpōmāyiṉ, mēlē kāṭṭiya meykaṇṭa nūlkaḷum piṟavum ettaṉaiyō vakaiyil evvaḷavō virivāka eḻutappaṭavēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷiya viḷaṅkukiṟatu. maṟu malarcci naṭaiyum putumuṟai veḷiyīṭum vēṇṭappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. muṉṉaiyiṉum paṭippōr tokaiyum, paṭippikkum kaḻakaṅkaḷum ippōtu perukiyiruttalāl, ivaṟṟiṟ kēṟpa, cittānta caiva nūlkaḷ peruki veḷiyātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu āṇṭu viḻāvilum aṟiñarkaḷāl vaṟpuṟuttappaṭ ṭirukkiṟatu.
putumuṟaiyil ivaiyēyaṉṟi ivaipōlum piṟavum ceyyavēṇṭumeṉpatil yāṉ karuttu vēṟupāṭu uṭaiyē ṉallēṉ. āyiṉum nam tamiḻakattil cila pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉ nikaḻntu pōnta camayappaṇikaḷai mīḷavum uyirppittu eḻacceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟi viḻaikiṉṟēṉ. iṉṟaiya nilaiyil appaṇi mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. ataṉāl namma variṭaiyē camayattuṟai paṟṟiya karuttu vēṟṟumai nīṅkumeṉṟu uṟuti kūṟalām.
13. camayappaṇikkuriya iṉṟai cūḻnilai
ippōtu nammiṭaiyē cātipaṟṟiyum, poruḷnilai paṟṟiyum, araciyaṟ karuttup paṟṟiyum pala vēṟu pirivukaḷ uṇṭākiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. cāti vēṟṟumaiyum camaya vēṟṟumaiyum tāṇṭavamāṭum nammiṭaiyē, kalvi vēṟṟumaiyum, kaṭci vēṟṟumaiyum pukuntu putup putu vēṟṟumaikaḷai mikukkiṉṟaṉa. iv vakaiyāl piḷavu perukukiṟatēyaṉṟi, oṟṟumai kāṇpatu ari tākiṟatu. paṇṭai nāḷil cāti vēṟṟumaiyūṭē camayamē oṟṟumai uṇṭu paṇṇiṟṟu. iṉṟu nām ataṉai maṟantōm.
iṉṟu, nammuṭaiya iḷaiñarkaḷ camayattuṟaiyil karuttu vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉar. camayanilaiyaṅkaḷil nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷiṟ kuṟṟaṅ kaṇṭu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. cila naṭai muṟaikaḷai vēṇṭā veṉa maṟukkiṉṟaṉar cila. ariya karuttukkaḷai ārāyccik kaṇkoṇṭu kaṇṭu kēṭ kiṉṟaṉar; avarkaḷaik kāṇavē nammavar palar aruvaruk kiṉṟaṉar. palar avar karuttaip puṟak kaṇikkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ avvāṟu māṟupaṭuvataṟkum vēṟupaṭu vataṟkum viṉāviṟkum kāraṇam yāteṉak kaṇṭaṟintavar mikac cilarē iruppar. avviḷaiñarkku nam kuṭumpaṅkaḷilō, kallūrikaḷilō camaya nilaiyaṅkaḷilō camayakkalvi tanta tuṇṭā? camaya nūṟ karuttukkaḷaiyēṉum nām kaṟpitta tuṇṭā? avarkaḷai vaituviṭuvatālō, puṟakkaṇip patālō, aruvaruppa tālō camayattuṟai paṇpaṭṭu viṭumā? camayavaṟivu vantu viṭumā? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavaṟṟai nām niṉaiyātu pōyiṉōm. pirintēkum kūṭṭattuṭ kūṭṭamāka avarkaḷaip pirippatutāṉē ceytiruk kiṉṟōm!
nām pirintatu pōtum. iṉi oṉṟupaṭuvōm. nammai oṉṟupaṭuttaṟkuc camayavuṇarvum camayavoḻukkamum peruntuṇai ceyyum. camayavuṇarvu oḻukkaṅkaḷukkuc camayak kalvivēṇṭum iṉṟaiya araciyalnilai camayak kalvi tarutaṟku oruppaṭātu; araciyal amaippu muṟaiyiṉai aṟintirukkiṉṟa nām, camayak kalvi vaḻaṅkumāṟu araciyalāraikkēṭpatum aṟamaṉṟu; muṟaiyu maṉṟu. ‘eṉ kaṭaṉ paṇi ceytu kiṭappatē’ eṉṟa nāvaraciṉ naṉmoḻi uḷḷattiṟpatiya vēṇṭum. nam kaṭaṉ paṇiceytu kiṭappatē eṉa attiru moḻi nam vāypāṭātal vēṇṭum.
14. camayappaṇi viḷakkam
camayappaṇi yeṉpatu yātu? iṉṟu nammiṭaiyē camayappaṇi yeṉpatu mikavum kuṟukiya ellaikku vantu viṭṭatu. tīkkai ceytu kōṭalum, aṉuṭṭāṉa civapūcaikaḷ ceyvatum, tiruk kōyilkaḷil nāṭpūcaṉai āṇṭuviḻāc ceyvatum, celvamikka pōtu tirukkōyil tiruppaṇi ceyvatumē camayappaṇikaḷākak karutappaṭuvaṉa vāyiṉa. ivai potuvāka vēṇṭuvaṉa eṉiṉum ciṟappāka uyirkaṭku vēṇṭum paṇikaḷaic ceyvatē camayap paṇiyākum. īcaṉukku aṉpu ceyyavēṇṭum eṉpatu camaya nūlkaḷ vaṟpuṟuttum pēraṟamākum. īcaṉukku aṉpu ceyvatāvatu avaṉuṭaiya aṭiyāriṭattu aṉpu ceyvatākum. aṭiyārāvār makkaḷuyir aṉaivarumāvār. ākavē makkaḷuyirkkuc cāti, niṟa, araciyal, camaya vēṟṟumai karutāmal nalaṉkaḷaic ceyvatu aṭiyavarpāl aṉpu celuttuvatākum. epporuḷaiyum ākkuvōṉ īcaṉ eṉavum, apporuḷai āḷumukattāl uytipeṟum makkaḷ aṭiyarāvar eṉavum cēkkiḻār perumāṉ, ñāṉacampantar aṟivuṟuttiyatāka eṭutturaippatu caivarkaḷ aṟintatākum. makkaḷuyirpāl uṇṭākum aṉpu piṉpu ellā vuyirk kaṇṇum cellum, evvuyirkkum aṉpu ceypavarē tammuyirkkum nalam ceypavarāvar. itaṉai “īcaṉukkaṉ pillār, evvuyirkkum aṉpillār evvuyirkkum aṉpillār tamakkum aṉpillār” eṉavum, ittakaiya aṉpu ceyyātāraip piṇaṅkaḷ eṉṟu ēcuvārāy, “pēcuvatu eṉ aṟivilāp piṇaṅkaḷai” eṉavum avarkaḷai, “nām iṇaṅkiṉ, piṟappilum iṟappiṉilum piṇaṅkiṭuvar” eṉavum aruṇanti civaṉār aṟaintu kūṟuvatu niṉaivu kūṟattakkatu. ivvāṟu evvuyirkkum aṉpuceytu vāḻvatē “vaiyattu vāḻvāṅkuvāḻum vāḻvākum; ivvāḻvu ṭaiyārē “vāṉuṟaiyum teyvamāvar” eṉat tiruvaḷḷu varum, “aṉpar paṇiceyya eṉai āḷākki viṭṭu viṭṭāl, iṉpa nilai tāṉē vanteytum” eṉat tāyumāṉārum viḷakkiyaruḷiṉar. ittakaiya cittānta vāḻvu nammiṭaiyē nilavukiṟatā eṉpatai iṟaivaṉ tirumuṉ niṉṟu nam neñcai nāmē ārāyntu teḷital vēṇṭum.
makkaḷuyirkkum, evvuyirkkum aṉpu ceytal vēṇṭum eṉiṉ, ataṉai eppaṭic ceyvatu eṉṟa viṉā eḻum. uyirkkup paṇiceyvateṉpatu uyirkku uṟuti payakkum kalviyaṟivum, uṭaṟku nalaṉ payakkum uṇavum maruntum utavutal ākum; ituvē aṉpu ceyvār ceyyum uyirp paṇiyākum; itaṉaic ceyyum camayamē innāḷil ulakameṅkum paraviyirukkiṟatu.
15. muṉṉāḷil caivampurinta camayappaṇi
ippaṇiyai nam caivam ceytatuṇṭā? itaṉaic ceyvatu camayappaṇiyeṉa nīṅkaḷē kūṟukiṉṟīrkaḷā? eṉṟu kēṭkalām. ikkēḷvi eḻutaṟkuk kāraṇam caiva camaya varalāṟu nam nāṭṭil paravāmaiyē eṉṟu kūṟalām. neṭuṅkāla ellaikkup pōka vēṇṭā. patiṉōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭaiyilē cōḻa vēntaruḷ vīrarācēntiraṉ eṉpāṉ, kaṅkaikoṇṭa cōḻapuratti liruntu āṭcipurintu varukaiyil nāṭṭil maḻaiyillāmaiyāl pañca muṇṭāyiṟṟu. akkālattē peruntuṉpattuk kuḷḷāṉa varkaḷil tañcai mānāṭṭil ālaṅkuṭip pakutiyiṉarāvar. appōtu ālaṅkuṭic civaṉ kōyilār 1011 kaḻañcu poṉṉum, 464 palam veḷḷiyum ūravarkkuk koṭuttu, uṇavukkum vitaikkum nel. peṟṟukkoḷḷumāṟu utaviṉar eṉa av vēntaṉuṭaiya kalveṭṭoṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. 1ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu mānāṭṭil vālācāpāttukku aṇmaiyil pālāṟṟaṅkaraiyil tirumukkūṭal eṉṉum ūr oṉṟu uḷatu. avvūril perumāḷ kōyilil caṉanāta maṇṭapameṉa oru maṇṭapamuṇṭu. ataṉ kaṇ, maruttuvac cālaiyoṉṟum vētam, carittiram, ilakkaṇam mutaliyaṉa kaṟpikkum kallūriyoṉṟum iruntaṉa. kallūri māṇavarkku uṟaiyuḷum, uṇavum, caṉikkiḻamaitōṟum eṇ ṇeyum, nāḷum iravil viḷakkerikka eṇṇeyum kōyil tantu utaviṟṟu. maruttuvac cālaiyil patiṉaintu paṭukkaikaḷ iruntaṉa. maruttuvar oruvar iraṇavaittiyar oruvar, maruntu ceytu koṭuppōr iruvar, cikiccaimakaḷir iruvar, potup paṇi ceyvōṉ oruvaṉ iruntaṉar. avarkku ōrāṇṭukkuriya maruntu vakaikaḷuḷ kōmūttira aritakai eṉa aritakai vakai kaḷum, kaṇṭīram, palā kōraṇṭa taiyilam, mutaliya tayila vakaikaḷum vilvāti kirutam, maṇṭūkaravaṭakam mutaliya vaccira kalpam, kaliyāṇa lavaṇam īṟākap pala maruntu vakaikaḷ aḷikkap paṭṭaṉa eṉa akkōyil kalveṭṭāṉṟu1 kūṟukiṟatu. tiruk kaṇṇa purattukkarukiluḷḷa tiruvirāmaṉatīccuram eṉṉum kōyi lār, taṅkaḷ kōyilukkirunta 50 vēli nilamum nāḷaṭaivil kuṟaintu pōkavē, “kōyil kāriyam” kuṉṟippōkak kaṇṭu, tillaip peruṅkōyil pēravai mākēccurarkku viṇṇappam ceytukoṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ uṇmaitērntu, cōḻamaṇṭalam, pāṇṭi, koṅku maṇṭalam, toṇṭai maṇṭalam, naṭunāṭu eṉṟa tamiḻ maṇṭalattiluḷḷa civaṉ kōyilkaḷuḷ 10 vēlikku mēṟpaṭṭa kōyilkaḷ āṇṭoṉṟukku oru kala ariciyum, maṟṟavai oru tūṇi orupatakkariciyum irāmaṉa tīccarattukkuk koṭukka vēṇṭumeṉap paṇittaṉar. itu tiripuvaṉa cakkara vartti irācarāca cōḻaṉ kālattup patiṉāṟā māṇṭil naṭantatu3 ivvāṟu civaṉ kōyilkaḷ naṭattiya centamiḻc caivapperumakkaḷ kalviyum uṇṭiyum maruntum kuruṭar, muṭavar aṉātaikaḷāṉa peṇṭir mutaliyōrukku uṟaiyuḷ tantu uṭaṟku uṇavum, uyirkkuk kalviyum tantu camayap paṇi purintuḷḷaṉar. ivaṟṟukkup pala nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa kalveṭṭukkaḷ cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa. ic camayappaṇikaḷai iṉṟu nam camaya nilaiyaṅkaḷ purikiṉṟaṉavā? illai yeṉpatai nām ellōrum aṟivōm. ippaṇikaḷaik kūṭiya aḷavil purintavarum kiṟittuva camayattavariṭaiyō iculām camayattavariṭaiyō camaya vakaiyil karuttu vēṟupāṭu koṇṭu piṇaṅkip pirintu niṟpavar uṇṭā? nam camayattavariṭaiyē iruppatu pōla avvaḷavu perun tokaiyiṉarāka uḷarā? ivaṟṟai nam camaya nilaiyaṅkaḷ eṇṇip pārkkiṉṟaṉavā? itu kāṟum pōṉatu pōkaṭṭum. iṉiyēṉum ic camayap paṇikaḷaip purivataṟku nammavar karut tūṉṟutal vēṇṭum.
16. caivattirumuṟaikaḷum caiva camaya nilaiyaṅkaḷum
iṟutiyāka oṉṟu kūṟi eṉ muṉṉuraiyai muṭikkiṉṟēṉ. nam nāṭṭuc civaṉkōyilkaḷuḷ mikka celvac ciṟappum tirup paṇic ciṟappumuṭaiya kōyilkaḷ cilavākum. avai ac ciṟappuṭaiyaṉa vāyiruttaṟkuk kāraṇam, camaya kuravarkaḷāṉa tiruñāṉa campantar mutaliyōrāl pāṭap peṟṟatēyākum. ceṭṭināṭṭuc celvamakkaḷāl tiruppaṇi ceyya peṟṟavai aṉaittum tirumuṟaip pārāṭṭu peṟṟavaiyēyākum. curuṅkac collumiṭattu icciṟappum celvamuṭaiya tirukkōyil kaṭku acciṟappum celvamum tantavai tirumuṟait tēvārat tirup pāṭṭukaḷē yākum ittirumuṟait tiruppāṭṭukkaḷai nāṭōṟu iṟaivaṉ tirumuṉ ōtavēṇṭumeṉa ovvoru tirukōyililum nam muṉṉōrāl nivantaṅkaḷ viṭappaṭṭaṉa. muṉṉaṇiyil ōtappaṭa vēṇṭiya tirumuṟaikaḷ ippōtu eṅkē uḷḷaṉa? civaparamporuḷaic centamiḻil vaḻipaṭṭa caivarkaḷ, iṉṟu anta moḻiyil tam vaḻipāṭaic ceykiṉṟaṉarā? ñāṉa campantar mutaliya caiva camaya kuravarkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa tamiḻ, iṉṟu caiva camaya nilaiyaṅkaḷil taṉ ciṟappiḻantu ōr otukkiṭattē otukik kiṭakkiṉṟatu. tillaip peruṅkōyilil maṇṇāḷu vēntaṉ vantu maṉaṅkaṉintu kaṇṭuvaṇaṅkip pōṟṟa takka nilaiyil irunta tirumuṟait tēvāram, centamiḻ caiva naṉmakkaḷāl civaṉ kōyilkaḷil puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. inta iḻi nilai oḻiya vēṇṭum.
iṉṟu araciyalavaiyil ārāyap peṟṟuvarum intu aṟanilaiyac caṭṭat tiruttam vantatum, ataṉkaṇ, ciṟanta nilaiyil iruttaṟkuriya tirumuṟaikaḷ iṭam peṟāmaiyai namcaivarkaḷ kāṇavē illai. tiruttam vaḻaṅkiya caiva naṉ makkaḷ tirumuṟaikaḷ mutaliṭam peṟa vēṇṭum eṉac ceppā toḻiyiṉum eḻuttilkūṭa eḻutiṉārillai “vēta neṟi taḻait tōṅka” vēta pārāyaṇam kuṟippiṭap paṭṭatu pōla, “caivat tuṟai viḷaṅka”, nam caiva tirumuṟaikaḷ kuṟikkappaṭavēṇṭumeṉa namnāṭṭu pattirikaikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum eḻutiṉōm; avaṟṟai avai veḷiyiṭāmalē iruṭṭaṭippuc ceytaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭut tamiḻk kōyilkaḷil tamiḻt tirumuṟaikaṭku iṭantara vēṇṭu meṉa aṭipaṇintu kaiyēnti niṟpatu tamiḻaṉukku māṉakkē ṭallavā eṉa naṇpar palar aṟivuṟuttiṉar, caivar palar camaya nilaiyaṅkaḷaip pātukāppavar pōlap pala kūṟak kāṇkiṉ ṟōmē yoḻiyat tamiḻt tirumuṟaikaṭkuc caivat tirukkōyil kaḷil mutaliṭam taravēṇṭu meṉṟō, camaya vaḻipāṭukaḷum tirumuṟai vaḻaṅkum centamiḻ vaḻiyāka naṭaipeṟa vēṇṭu meṉṟō nāṭaṟiya moḻikiṉṟilar.
17. caiva cittānta āciriyarkaḷ
vētam pacu eṉṟum, ākamam ataṉ pāl eṉṟum, mutalvar ōtum tamiḻ ataṉ neyyeṉṟum, meykaṇṭār vaḻaṅkiya civañāṉa pōtam anneyyiṉ cuvaiyeṉṟum caivarkaḷ kūṟuvar. itaṉāl, meykaṇṭār vaḻaṅkiya meynnūl, nālva rōtum tamiḻ, pāl maṇakkum ñāṉamātal eṉpatu orutalai. nālvarōtun tamiḻai naṭṭāṟṟil eṟintu viṭṭu, meykaṇṭa ñāṉa nūlai maṭṭil kaippaṟṟi ñāṉattaippeṟa muyaṉṟa camaya nilaiyaṅkaḷum nāṭṭil uṇṭu. anta ñāṉam aṭippaṭai yillāta ōviyakkūṭamāy uruvākātoḻintatai nāṭaṟiyum.
iṉi, ñāṉa nūlpāl pērārvamuṭaiyār palar nam nāṭṭil kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. ñāṉa nūliṉpāl eḻum ārvam uṇmai yāṉāl, atu anta nūlai vaḻaṅkiya ñāṉāciriyar pāl ayarā aṉpiṉaiyuṇṭupaṇṇum; avar piṟanta vūrkkut tam piṟappil oru muṟaiyēṉum pōyvara vēṇṭumeṉṟa viḻaivait tōṟṟu vikkum avar piṟantavūr, piṟanta maṉai mutaliyavaṟṟaip palarum uṇṭu makiḻattakka ūkkattai uṇṭākkum. mey kaṇṭār piṟanta peṇṇākaṭamō, avar piṟanta maṉaiyirunta kaḷappāḷar mēṭō kaṇṭavar cilar. avar irunta tiruveṇṇey nallūril irukkum meykaṇṭār. tirumaṭattaik kāṇpavar, caivar kaḷākiya nammai oru muṟaiyēṉum caliyā toḻiyār. mey kaṇṭār nilaiyē ituvāyiṉ, avaruṭaiya māṇavarum civañāṉa citti vaḻaṅkiyavarumāṉa aruṇanti civaṉāruṭaiya tiruttuṟaiyūr celpavar accilariṉum cilar eṉpatu tēṟṟam. maṟṟavaraip paṟṟik kūṟuvāṉēṉ?
itu kiṭakka, camayakuravarkaḷāṉa ñāṉacampantar piṟanta maṉai orupuṟam irukka, avar ūr peyar pukaivaṇṭi nilaiyattil evvāṟu uḷatu? tiṉṉavelli tirunelvēli yāka, tiruc ciṉṉāppaḷḷi tiruccirāppaḷḷiyāka, tiṉṉaṉūr tiruniṉṟavūrāka, nikappaṭṭām nākaippaṭṭiṉamākat tiruntum innāḷilum, ñāṉa campantar piṟanta cīkāḻi ṣiyyāḷi eṉṟē iṉṉum nilavu kiṟatu. avvūril naṭanta caiva cittānta camācamum atu tiruttap paṭa vēṇṭumeṉat tīrmāṉam ceytatu. aḵtu iṉṟu varai tiruntāmaikkuk kāraṇam caivaruḷḷattil caiva
vuṇarvu naṉku patiyāmai yaittāṉē kāṭṭukiṟatu! iṉṟu nam nāṭṭil ciṟantu viḷaṅkum camayattavaruḷ kiṟittavarum iculāmiyarum taṅkaḷ camaya mutalvar piṟanta vūrkaḷai ettuṇaip perumitattōṭu virumpi nōkkukiṉṟaṉar avarkaḷaik kaṇṭēṉum caivarkaḷākiya nām tirunta vēṇṭāvā? naṭu nāṭṭaip pāṭavanta cēkkiḻār perumāṉ, atu, “aṟantarunāvuk karacum, ālālacuntararum piṟantaruḷa” varum perumaiyuṭaiya teṉa ettuṇaip perumitat tōṭu kūṟukiṉṟār! itaṉāl, periyōr piṟappāl uḷatākum perumai kaṇṭu perumitaṅkoḷvatu caivarkaṭkup piṟappiyal pākat tāṉē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu? apperumitam iṉṟu maṟaintu kiṭappatu ākātu. cīkāḻiyum tiruvāmūrum, tirunāvalūrum tiruvātavūrum centamiḻc caivap perumakkaḷ vāḻvil oru muṟaiyēṉum kāṇṭal vēṇṭum; atuvaḻiyāka eytum iṉpamum camayappaṟṟum nammaic camayattuṟaiyil araṇ ceyyum.
18. camayac coṟpoḻivukaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippu
muṭivāka oṉṟu. camayac cārpāka nikaḻum viḻākkaḷilum, kaḻaka nikaḻccikaḷilum camayac coṟpoḻivu kaḷ nikaḻttap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. coṟpoḻivukkuriya poruḷ kaḷuḷ tiruttoṇṭar varalāṟukaḷum purāṇavaralāṟukaḷum eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. kaviiravīntiranāt tākūr kūṟiyatu pōla ikkālam kāraṇa kāriya ārāycci puriyuṅ kālamātaliṉ avaṟṟai mikka cemmaiyākak kūṟa vēṇṭum. tiruttoṇṭar varalāṟukaḷuḷ oruvar taṉit tiruntu paṭittuc cintittaṟkuriya varalāṟukaḷum, piṟarkku eṭutturaittaṟkuriya varalāṟukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. tiruk kaḷiṟṟup paṭiyār vakutturaikkum valviṉai varalāṟukaḷum melviṉai varalāṟukaḷum pakuttaṟintu melviṉai varalāṟu kaḷaiyē coṟpoḻivuk kuriyavākak kōṭal ciṟappu. in nilaiyil, ñāṉacampantar, nāvaracar, nampiyārūrar, kāraik kālammaiyār, cēramāṉ perumāḷ eṉṟa inta aivar varalāṟu kaḷaiyum avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya tiruppāṭṭukkaḷukkuc ciṟappaḷittup poruḷ viḷakkam ceytum coṟpoḻivu ceyvatu ciṟanta neṟiyākum. purāṇavaralāṟukaḷuḷ kaṭavuḷarai makkaḷiṉum kīḻāṉavarākak kāṭṭum varalāṟu kaḷaip paṭakkāṭcikaḷilum nāṭaka mēṭaikaḷilum kāṭṭā toḻiyumāṟu caivarkaḷākiya nām ēṟpāṭu ceytal vēṇṭum. avai vēṇṭappaṭṭa kālam vēṟu, ikkāla nilaivēṟu eṉpatai naṉku niṉaivu kūra vēṇṭum.
19. muṭippurai
perumakkaḷē, eṉatu talaimaiyurai mika nīṇṭuviṭṭa teṉpatai naṉku uṇarukiṟēṉ. uṅkaḷ poṟumaiyiṉai mikavum alaittuviṭṭēṉ. āṉāl, iccaiva cittānta capaiyiṉ tōṟṟattukku amainta cūḻ nilaiyiṉaiyum, itu ceyta tiruppaṇiyiṉaiyum ataṉāl viḷainta payaṉaiyum mutaṟkaṇ kūṟa vēṇṭiya kaṭamai vantatu. piṉṉarccaiva cittāntameṉpatiṉ poruḷai viḷakkiṉēṉ. ataṉpiṉ, ikkālattil viññāṉa vaḷarcciyiṉ payaṉāy makkaḷ uḷḷam eytiyirukkum nilai yiṉaiyum, annilaiyil tattuva ñāṉam irukkum iruppiṉaiyum, ataṉ muṭivil camaya ñāṉam viḷaṅkum cālpiṉaiyum kāṭṭi, avai pacu pāca pati ñāṉaṅkaḷāyp paṭimuṟaiyil amain tirukkum amaippiṉaiyum, mēṉāṭukaḷil avai eytiyirukkum iyalpiṉaiyum, eṭuttuk kūṟa vēṇṭiyavaṉāyiṉēṉ. piṉpu caiva cittāntam, ēṉaiya nam nāṭṭuc cittāntaṅkaḷōṭu ottum, uyarntum irukkum nalattaik kūṟiṉēṉ. itaṉ kaṇ cilar kūṟum taṭaikaṭku ōraḷavu viṭaiyaḷittēṉ. ic caiva cittāntak karuttukkaḷai nam nāṭṭiṟ parapputaṟku vēṇṭum muṟaiyiṉaik kūṟumāṟṟāl, camayap paṇiyirukka vēṇṭiya muṟaiyiṉaiyum, iṭaikkālac cōḻapāṇṭiyar kālattil camaya aṟa nilaiyaṅkaḷ purinta camayap paṇiyiṉaik kalveṭṭukkaḷiṉ tuṇai koṇṭu eṭuttōti attakaiya paṇikaḷai mīḷavum nammuṭaiya nilaiyaṅkaḷ puriya vēṇṭumeṉa vēṇṭiṉēṉ. piṉpu civaṉ kōyilkaḷil tirumuṟaikaḷ irukkum nilaiyiṉaiyum, itaṟkiṭaiyē ivaṟṟaiyum ivaṟṟait toṭarntu pōnta cittānta nūlkaḷaiyum āciriyarkaḷ pāl caivarkaḷākiya nām kāṭṭum aṉpiṉmaiyiṉaiyum, kāṭṭa vēṇṭiya aṉpu neṟiyiṉaiyum, camayac coṟpoḻivu ceyyu miṭattu nām kuṟikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya karuttukkaḷaiyum ōraḷavu kāṭṭiṉēṉ. ivvaḷavum amaitiyōṭu kēṭṭuc ciṟappitta uṅkaḷ peruntakaimaikku eṉ vaṇakkattait terivittu eṉṉai innilaiyil vaittuc ciṟappikkum ic capait talaivarkaḷ, ceyalāḷarkaḷ aṉaivarukkum eṉ vaṇakkattai mikka paṇi vōṭu terivittuk koṇṭu, eṉṉaiyum uṅkaḷaiyum ellō raiyum oṉṟu kūṭṭi nam caivacittāntap perumporuḷai niṉaintu. iṉpuṟu vikkum civakuru nātaṉākiya
“teṉtamiḻkkalai terintavar poruntiya tēvūr
  aṉpaṉ cēvaṭi aṭaintaṉam allal oṉṟilamē”
- tiruñāṉacampantar.
eṉṟu kūṟi muṭikkiṟēṉ. naṉṟi vaṇakkam.


12. civañāṉapōtac cemporuḷ
centilāṇṭavaṉ tuṇai tiruveṇṇeynallūr
meykaṇṭa tēvar aruḷicceyta
civañāṉa pōtac cūttiramum
āṅkila moḻi peyarppum*
kāppu
	kallāl niḻalmalai, villār aruḷi
	pollār iṇaimalar, nallār puṉaivarē.
The Good will crown their heads with the two Feet of Ganesha who was begotten by the Great Teacher, who sat under the Sacred Mountain Banyan tree and removed the doubts of the Great Nandi. 
nūl
mutaṟ cūttiram
pati uṇmaikkup piramāṇam
avaṉ avaḷ atueṉum avaimū viṉaimaiyiṉ
tōṟṟiya titiyē oṭuṅkimalat tuḷatām
antam āti eṉmaṉār pulavar.
As the (seen) universe, spoken of as he she and it. undergoes three changes (origin, development and decay), this must be an entity created (by an efficient cause). This entity owing to its conjunction with Anava Mala has to emanate from Hara to whom it returns during Samharam. Hence, the learned say that Hara is the First Cause.
iraṇṭāñ cūttiram
pāca uṇmaikkup piramāṇam
	avaiyē tāṉē yāyiru viṉaiyiṉ
	pōkku varavu puriya āṇaiyiṉ
	nīkkam iṉṟi niṟkum aṉṟē.
He is one with the souls (Abetha). he is different from them (Betha). He is one and different from them (betha betha). He stands in Samava union with His Gnana Sakti and causes the souls to undergo the processes of evolution (births) and return (Samharam) by including their good and bad acts (Karma).
mūṉṟāñ cūttiram
pacu uṇmaikkup piramāṇam
	uḷatuilatu eṉṟaliṉ eṉatuṭal eṉṟaliṉ
	aimpulaṉ oṭukkam aṟitaliṉ kaṇpaṭil
	uṇṭiviṉai iṉmaiyiṉ uṇartta uṇartaliṉ
	māyā iyantira taṉuviṉuḷ āṉmā.
It rejects every portion of the body as not being itself; it says my body; it is conscious of dreams; it exists in sleep without feeling pleasure or pain or movements; it knows from others; this is the soul which exists in the body formed as a machine from Maya.
nāṉkāñ cūttiram
pacuviṉ potu ilakkaṇam
	antak karaṇam avaṟṟiṉoṉ ṟaṉṟuavai
	cantittatu āṉmāc cakacamalat tuṇarātu
	amaiccaracu ēyppaniṉṟu añcavat taittē.
The soul is not one of the Andakaranas It is not conscious when it is in conjunction with Anavamala. It becomes conscious only when it meets the Andakarana, just as a king understands through his ministers. The relation of the soul to the fvie Avastha is also similar.
aintāñ cūttiram
pāca ilakkaṇam
	viḷampiya uḷḷattu meyvāy kaṇmūkku
	aḷantaṟintu aṟiyā āṅkavai pōlat
	tāmtam uṇarviṉ tamiyaruḷ
	kāntaṅ kaṇṭa pacācat tavaiyē.
The senses while perceiving the object cannot perceive themselves or the soul; and they are perceived by soul. Similarly, the soul while perceiving cannot perceive itself (while thinking cannot think thought) and God. It is moved by the Arul Sakti of God, as the magnet moves the iron, while Himself remains immoveable or unchangeable.
āṟāñ cūttiram
pati ilakkaṇam
	uṇaruru acatteṉiṉ uṇarātu iṉmaiyiṉ
	irutiṟaṉ allatu civacat tāmeṉa
	iraṇṭu vakaiyiṉ icaikkumaṉ ulakē.
That which is perceived by the senses is Asat (changeable). That which is not so perceived does not exist. God is neither the one nor the other, and hence called Siva Sat or Chit Sat by the wise; Chit or Siva when not understood by the human intelligence and Sat when perceived with divine wisdom.
ēḻāñ cūttiram
pacuviṉ ciṟappu ilakkaṇam
		yāvaiyum cūṉiyam cattetir ākaliṉ
		cattē aṟiyātu acattilatu aṟiyātu
		irutiṟaṉ aṟivuḷatu iraṇṭalā āṉmā.
In the presence of  Sat, every thing elese (cosmos-Asat) is Sunyam (non-apparent). Hence Sat cannot perceive Asat. As Asat does not exist, it cannot perceive Sat. That which perceives both, cannot be either of them. This is the soul (called Satasat).
eṭṭāñ cūttiram
ñāṉam uṇarum muṟai
	aimpula vēṭariṉ ayarntaṉai vaḷarnteṉat
	tammutal kuruvumāyt tavattiṉil uṇarttaviṭṭu
	aṉṉiyam iṉmaiyiṉ araṉkaḻal celumē.
The Lord appearing as Guru to the soul which had advanced in Tapas (virtue and knowledge) instructs him that he has wasted himself by living among the savages of the five senses; and on this, the soul, understanding its real nature, leaves its former associates and being different from Him, becomes united to His Feet.
oṉpatāñ cūttiram
āṉma cutti
	ūṉakkaṇ pācam uṇarāp patiyai
	ñāṉak kaṇṇiṉiṟ cintai nāṭi
	urāttuṇait tērtteṉap pācam oruvat
	taṇṇiḻa lāmpativiti eṇṇumañ ceḻuttē.
The soul, on perceiving in itself with the eye of Gnanam, the Lord who cannot be perceived by the human intellect or senses, and on giving up the world (Pasa) by knowing it to be false as a mirage, will find its rest in the Lord. Let the soul contemplate Sri Panchatchara according to Law.
pattāñ cūttiram
pāca nīkkam
	avaṉē tāṉē ākiya anneṟi
	ēka ṉāki iṟaipaṇi niṟka
	malamāyai taṉṉoṭu valviṉai iṉṟē.
As the Lord becomes one with the soul in its human condition, so let the soul become one with Him and perceive all its actions to be His. Then will it lose all its Mala, Maya and Karma.
patiṉōrāñ cūttiram
civappēṟu
	kāṇuṅ kaṇṇukkuk kāṭṭum uḷampōl,
	kāṇauḷ ḷattaik kaṇṭu kāṭṭaliṉ
	ayarā aṉpiṉ araṉkaḻal celumē.
As the Soul enables the eye to see and itself sees, so Hara enables the soul to know and itself knows. And this Adwaitha knowledge and undying Love will unite it to His Feet.
paṉṉiraṇṭāñ cūttiram
cīvaṉ muttariṉ taṉmai
	cemmalar nōṉṟāḷ cēral oṭṭā
	ammalaṅ kaḻīi aṉparoṭu marīi
	mālaṟa nēyam malintavar vēṭamum
	ālayan tāṉum araṉeṉat toḻumē.
Let the Jivathma after washing off its Mala which separates it from the strong Lotus Feet of the Lord and mixing in the society of Bhaktas (Jivan Muktas) whose souls abound with love, having lost dark ignorance, contemplate their Forms and the Forms in the temples as His Form.
civañāṉa pōtam
potu atikāram	uṇmai atikāram
	piramāṇa	ilakkaṇa	cātaṉa	payaṉ
	iyal	iyal	iyal	iyal
	(1,2,3 cūt.)	(4,5,6 cūt.)	(7,8,9 cūt)	(10,11,12 cūt.)
			
civañāṉa pōta mūlamum
āṅkila moḻi peyarppum
muṟṟiṟṟu.



civañāṉa pōtac cemporuḷ
kāppu
	kallāl niḻalmalai, villār aruḷiya
	pollār iṇaimalar, nallār puṉaivarē.
nam kaṇṇukkup pulaṉākum ivvulakam, uyiruṭaip poruḷ uyiril poruḷ eṉavum, iyaṟkaip poruḷ ceyaṟkaip poruḷ eṉavum, parup poruḷ nīriyaṟ poruḷ kāliyaṟ poruḷ eṉavum, ceyyappaṭum ārāyccik kēṟpa vakuttu ārāyappaṭum. camayattuṟaiyil niṉṟu ārāycci ceyvōr taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ camayak koḷkaikaṭ kēṟpap poruḷkaḷaip pakuttu ārāyvar. tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlōr poruḷkaḷai makkaṭ poruḷ eṉṟum piṟa poruḷ eṉṟum pakuttu nōkkuvar. ivvulakiṭai niṉṟu aṟivārāycci ceyyum aṟiñar aṉaivarum tamakkēṟṟa vakaiyil ivvulakam pakuttu nōkkappaṭum pāṉmaiyuṭaiyatāyiruppataik kāṇkiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ nūṉ muṟaip paṭi ivvulakam, uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai eṉa iruvakaiyāka vaittup poru ḷārāycci ceyyumāṟu amaintuḷatu. uyartiṇaip poruḷ āṇ eṉṟum, peṇ eṉṟum pakukkappaṭukiṟatu; ēṉai aḵṟiṇaip poruḷiṉkaṇ uyiruṭaiyaṉa cila āṇ peṇṇeṉap pakuttuṇarap paṭiṉum uyiruṭaip poruḷuṭ cilavum, uyiril poruḷ aṉaittum āṇ peṇ pākupāṭṭiṟku akappaṭuvatillai; ataṉāl apporuḷ atu eṉṟa collālum, uyartiṇai āṇ avaṉ eṉṟacollālum, uyartiṇaip peṇ avaḷ eṉṟa collālum kuṟikkappaṭuvatu tamiḻ paṭippavar aṉaivarum aṟintatu. 
itaṉāl ivvulaku muṟṟum avaṉ, avaḷ, atu eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷiṉ tokutiyē yeṉpatu teḷivām. āṉatu paṟṟiyē meykaṇṭa tēvar ivvulakattai, "avaṉ avaḷ atu eṉum avai" eṉṟu kuṟittaruḷiṉār. āṇum peṇṇumāyp palar kūṭiyirukkum iṭam avai yeṉappaṭum. avvāṟē, avaṉ, avaḷ, atu eṉṉum mūvakaip poruḷum kūṭi iyaṉṟuḷḷa ulakum avai yeṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.
nām kāṇum ovvōr avaiyum makkaḷ vantu kūṭuṅkāl tōṟṟamum, irukkuṅkāl nilai pēṟum, pirintu pōkuṅkāl kēṭum eytukiṟatu. itupōlavē ivvulakamākiya avaiyum tōṟṟam, nilai, iṟuti yeṉṟa mūṉṟu ceyalkaḷaiyum uṭaiya teṉpatu iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. nām kāṇum avaikaḷ tāmē kūṭuvatiṉṟi, oruvar muṉṉiṉṟu kūṭṭak kūṭalum, irutta iruttalum, pirikkap piritalum uṭaiyatu; itupōlavē iv vulakamum tāṉē tōṉṟātu mutalvaṉ oruvaṉ tōṟṟuvikkat tōṉṟalum, niṟpikka nilaipeṟutalum, oṭukka oṭuṅkutalum uṭaiyateṉpatu tāṉē viḷaṅkum. nammāṟ kāṇappaṭum ulakam, oruvar kūṭṭak kūṭiyirukkum avai pōlvatākaiyāl, oruvar tōṟṟuvikkat tōṉṟi nilaipeṟukiṟatu eṉpatu teḷivām. ātalāl, āciriyar, avaṉum avaḷum atuvumākiya poruḷkaḷ kūṭiyirukkum ivvulaka mākiya avaikkut tōṉṟutal mutaliya mūviṉaikaḷ1 irukkiṟapaṭiyāl, oruvar kūṭṭak kūṭiyirukkum avaipōla, ulakam tōṟṟuvikkat tōṉṟi nilai peṟukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. itaṉai mutaṟcūttiram, avai mūviṉai uṭaimaiyiṉ tōṟṟiya titiyē eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. itu, tāṉē tōṉṟiya taṉṟu eṉpataṟkākat tōṉṟiya titi eṉṟu collāmal tōṟṟiya titi eṉṟār. ataṟku, tāṉē tōṉṟāmal tōṟṟu vikkappaṭṭu uḷatāyatu eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟappaṭum.
ulakil nām kūṭṭak kūṭum avai muṭintavaḻi, avva vaiyiṟ kūṭiyiruntōr tattam maṉaikkuc ceṉṟu oṭuṅkuvar; mīḷa nām kūṭṭumpōtu  avarkaḷ tām oṭuṅkiyirunta maṉaiyiliruntu vantu kūṭuvar. ivvaṇṇamē ivvulakamākiya avaiyum oṭuṅkum pōtu iṟaivaṉiṭattilē ceṉṟoṭuṅki, mīḷat tōṟṟuvik kumpōtu avaṉiṭattiruntē vantu ulakamākiya avaiyāyk kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu. ulakattai oṭukkum pōtu, aḵtu oṭuṅkiyiruppataṟkut tāṉē iṭamātalāl, iṟaivaṉai "oṭuṅki" eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟukiṉṟār. oṭuṅkiya ulakam tāṉ oṭuṅkiyiruppataṟku iṭamāṉa oṭuṅkiyiliruntē tōṉṟuvatu koṇṭu. "oṭuṅki uḷatām " eṉṟār. eṉavē, avaṉ avaḷ atu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tokutiyākiya avai tōṟṟiya titiyāyk kāṭciyaḷittu oṭuṅkum eṉṟum, tāṉ oṭuṅkiyirunta oṭuṅki yiṉiṉṟē mīḷa uḷatām eṉṟum terivitta vāṟāyiṟṟu.
orunāḷ kūṭiya oru pēravai maṟunāḷum mīḷakkūṭumāyiṉ, nām eṉṉa niṉaippōm? muṉṉāḷ kūṭiyataṉ nōkkam kaikūṭāmaiyāl maṟunāḷum kūṭi yirukkiṟa teṉṟum, kaikūṭāmaikkuk kāraṇamāyirunta taṭai muṉṉāḷē nīṅkātu maṟunāḷ kūṭuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟ ṟeṉṟum tāṉē niṉaippōm. atu pōlavē, orukāl tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭa ulakam, tōṉṟiyatukoṇṭu peṟṟuk koḷḷa vēṇṭiya payaṉaip peṟṟuk koḷḷāmaiyāl oṭuṅki mīḷat tōṟṟuvikkappaṭutāyiṟṟeṉṟum, peṟṟuk koḷḷāvāṟu taṭuttirunta taṭai yiṉāl mīḷat tōṟṟuvikkap paṭukiṟa teṉṟum eṇṇavēṇṭum. attaṭaiyātu eṉa ārāyntu aḵtāvatu malam eṉṟu kaṇṭataṉāl, ulakamākiya avai oṭuṅki malattāl mīḷa avvoṭuṅ kiyiṉiṉṟē uḷatām eṉṟu āciriyar aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār.
ivvāṟu oṭukkuvatum oṭuṅkiyataṟku iṭamum ātalāl iṟaivaṉ ulakukku antamum, mīḷat tōṟṟuvittaṟkum avaṉē kāraṇamātalāl ātiyumākiṉṟāṉ. taṉakku ātiyum antamum illāta iṟaivaṉ, ulakiṟku antamum ātiyumāvatu paṟṟi, "antam āti eṉmaṉār pulavar" eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟukiṉṟār. eṉmaṉār pulavar eṉpataṟku, eṉṟu colluvar ārāycciyuṇarvu paṭaitta aṟiñar eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
iccūttirattāl cila karuttukkaḷ teḷivupaṭa vēṇṭum. ōr avai kūṭṭap peṟutaṟku oru nōkkam iruppatu pōla, ulakap paṭaippukkum oru nōkkam iruttal vēṇṭum. annōkkam yātu? annōkkam kaikūṭātu taṭukkum malamyātu? ulakap paṭaippu annōkkattai evvāṟu kaikūṭṭuvikkum?
ivvulakam uyirum uyiril poruḷumākak kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu. ivvāṟu kāṭciyaḷikkac ceyvatutāṉ ulakap paṭaippu eṉpatu. uyirkaḷ ākkappaṭuvatu millai; aḻivatumillai. iṟaivaṉ eṉṟu uṇṭō aṉṟē uyirkaḷ uṇṭu. iṟaivaṉ oruvaṉ; uyirkaḷ eṇṇiṟantaṉa. cempiṉ kaṇ kaḷimpu uṭaṉiruppatu pōla uyirkaḷiṭattē malam uṭaṉ irukkiṟatu. kaḷimpu nīṅkiṉa cempu oḷiviṭṭut tikaḻvatupōla, malam nīṅkiṉa uyir ñāṉavoḷi ciṟantu pēriṉpam eytum. cempiṉkaṇ kaḷimpu eppoḻutu ēṉ uṇṭāyiṟ ṟeṉṟa kēḷvikku viṭai kiṭaiyātu; atu pōla uyir kaṭku malam eppoḻutu ēṉ uṇṭāyiṟ ṟeṉṟu kāṇpatu ākāta oṉṟu. itaṉai aṉāti campantam eṉṟu aṟivunūlōr kūṟuvar. uyiriṉ vēṟākak kāṭciyaḷikkum uṭalum karuvikaḷum nukar poruḷumākiya ulakam ivvāṟu kāṇappaṭumāṟu ceyvatutāṉ paṭaippu eṉpatu. ivvulakattukku mutaṟkāraṇam oṉṟu uṇṭu. uyiril poruḷākiya ivvulakam oṭuṅkuṅkāl mutalil nuṇporuḷāy oṭuṅkum; atuvē piṉṉar āṟṟalāy (Energy) oṭuṅkum; anta āṟṟal (cakti) māyai yeṉṟu nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum. iṟaivaṉ oḷiyum oḷip poruḷum pōlvaṉ. oḷipporuḷiṉiṉṟum nīṅkāmal irukkum oḷipōla avaṉiṭat tiṉiṉṟum nīṅkāmal irup patu avaṉatu aruḷ. aḵtu aruṭcatti yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. āṟṟalāy oṭuṅkiya māyai avaṉatu aruṭcattiyil taṅkum, anta aruṭcatti iṟaivaṉākiya civattil oṭuṅkiyirukkum oṭuṅyākiya civaṉ, māyaiyai mīḷat tōṟṟuvikkak karutuvāṉāyiṉ, aruṭcattiyai nōkkuvaṉ; atu māyaiyākiya caktiyai veḷippaṭutti, uṭampum karuviyum poruḷum ulakumāka virintu kāṭcip paṭac ceyyum. ataṉai itupaṟṟiyē, “oṭuṅkiyuḷatām” eṉṟār.
mutaṟ kāraṇamākak koṇṭu uyirkaṭkariya uṭampoṭu kūṭi vāḻum uyir malamaṟaippu nīṅki aṟivu viḷaṅkap peṟukiṟatu. uyirkku anta naṉṉilai eyta vēṇṭumeṉṟa pēraruḷāl ivvulakam mīḷap paṭaikkappaṭukiṟatu. aṟivuruvāka irukkum uyirōṭu koṇṭa aṉāti campantattāl, malam ataṉ aṟivai maṟaittu, ulakiṭait tōṉṟip peṟutaṟkuriya aṟivu viḷakkattai nirampap peṟātavāṟu taṭai ceykiṉṟatu. aṟivu viḷakkam peṟṟu ataṉ vaḻiyāka eytum pēriṉpattai nukarvatutāṉ uyiriṉ nōkkam; ataṟkut tuṇaipurivatu, uṭal karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku mutaṟ kāraṇamākiya māyai. ik karuttukkaḷ viḷaṅkavē, āciriyar meykaṇṭār,
“	avaṉ avaḷ atueṉum avaimū viṉaimaiyiṉ
 	tōṟṟiya titiyē oṭuṅkimalat tuḷatām
	antam āti eṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉṟu aṟivuṟut taruḷukiṉṟār.
2. avaṉ avaḷ atu eṉṉum mūvakaip poruḷum kūṭik kāṭciyaḷikkum ulakamākiya avaiyait tōṟṟuvittu nilai peṟuvittaṟku iṟaivaṉ taṉatu aruṭcattiyai nōkkuvaṉ eṉṟu muṉṉē kaṇṭōm. anta aruṭcatti iṟaivaṉākiya mutalvaṉukku āṇai eṉṟu collappaṭum. avaṉ niṉaippaiyē taṉakku āṇaiyākak koṇṭu avvaruṭcatti iyaṅkuvatāl aḵtu āṇai eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. anta āṇaiyum iṟaivaṉum, kuṇamum kuṇiyum pōlap pirintu nillāta taṟkiḻamait toṭarpu uṭaiyavai; centāmaraip pūviṉ cemmai pirikkamuṭiyātu oṉṟiyiruppatu pōla iṟaivaṉatu āṇaiyākiya aruṭ catti avaṉiṟ piriyatu oṉṟi niṟkum. itupaṟṟiyē āciriyar, antam ātiyeṉa mutaṟ cūttirattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa iṟaivaṉākiya mutalvaṉ “āṇaiyiṉ nīkkamiṉṟi niṟkum” eṉṟa aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār, nīṅki niṟpataṟkum nīkkamiṉṟi niṟpataṟkum urimaiyuṭaiyavaṉ mutal vaṉātalāl, āṇai nīkkamiṉṟi niṟkum eṉṟu collāmal, mutalvaṉ “āṇaiyiṉiṉṟum nīkkam iṉṟi niṟkum ” eṉṟu colliyaruḷukiṉṟār.
iṉi, ivvulakattukku antamum ātiyumāy niṟkum iṟaivaṉ irukkum iyalpu kūṟaluṟṟa āciriyar, ulakamākiya avaiyēyāy, tāṉēyāy, avaiyē tāṉēyāy niṟkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. mutalvaṉ tāṉēyāy, avaiyēyāy niṟkum, avai yāyum tāṉāyum uṭaṉ niṟkum eṉpatu karuttu. mutalvaṉ tāṉēyāy niṟpateṉpatu, mutalvaṉ ulakuyirkaḷōṭu kalakkāmal vēṟāyt taṉittu niṟkum nilai; avaiyēyātaleṉpatu, ulakamākiya avai vēṟu tāṉ vēṟu eṉṉātavāṟu oṉṟāyk kalantu niṟkum nilai; uyirum uṭampumāyk kalantu nilavum nampāl, uyir vēṟu uṭampu vēṟu eṉap pirittuk kāṇātapaṭi nammuyir uṭampōṭu kalantu oṉṟāy niṟpatu pōla, mutalvaṉ ulakil oṉṟāyk kalantu niṟkiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu. ulakil oṉṟāyk kalantu niṟkuṅkāl ulakam mutalvaṉātalum, mutalvaṉ ulakamātalum illai. evvāṟeṉṟāl, uṭampōṭu oṉṟāyk kalantu niṟkum uyir uṭampāvatum, uṭampu uyirāvatum illai yaṉṟō, atupōla eṉa aṟital vēṇṭum. ulakil nilavum uyirkaṭku, aṟivu viḻaivu ceyal eṉṟa mūṉṟu iyalpukaḷ uṇṭu. aṟivu mutaliya iyalpukaḷ veḷippaṭutaṟkuriya karuviyoṭu kūṭumāyiṉ, uyir, aṟivatō viḻaivatō ceyvatō mēṟkoḷḷum. uyiriṉ vēṟāya ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaṭku attakaiya ceyal kiṭaiyātu. apporuḷkaḷ uyirpporuṭṭuc ceyaṟpaṭavēṇṭi yiruppatāl mutalvaṉ avaṟṟōṭu oṉṟāyk kalantu niṟkiṉṟāṉ. avvāṟu nillāṉāyiṉ, ulakiyaṟ poruḷ oru neṟippaṭa oḻukutaṟku ētu illaiyākum; ikkāla viññāṉa vaḷarc cikkum iṭamillaiyām.
ulakiyaṟ poruḷāl ākiya uṭampiṉkaṇ niṟkum uyir malat taṭaiyiliruntu nīṅka vēṇṭumeṉṟu, ulaka vāḻkkaiyait toṭaṅkum; vāḻkkaiyil, aṟivaṉavaṟṟai aṟiyavum, viḻaiyavum, ceyyavum iṟaivaṉ uṭaṉiruntu tuṇai ceykiṉṟāṉ. uṭampōṭu oṉṟāyum, uyiriṉ vēṟāyum, iraṇṭiṉaiyum viṭṭu nīṅkāmalum, iruppatāl avvāṟu irukkum mutalvaṉatu iruppu uṭaṉātal eṉppaṭukiṟatu; iraṇṭām cūttiram, avaiyē tāṉē yāy eṉṟu uṇarttukiṟatu. ulakiṟku antam ceyyum ātiyākiya mutalvaṉ antamum ātiyumākumpōtu vēṟāy niṟpatum, uyiril poruḷākiya ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷōṭu oṉṟāy niṟpatum, uyirkaḷōṭu uṭaṉāy niṟpatum uṇartta vanta tiruñāṉa campantap perumāṉ,
“īṟāymutal oṉṟāyiru peṇṇāṇkuṇa mūṉṟāy
 māṟāmaṟai nāṉkāyvaru pūtammavai aintāy
 āṟārcuvai ēḻōcaiyo ṭeṭṭutticai tāṉāy
 vēṟāyuṭa ṉāṉāṉ iṭam vīḻimmiḻa laiyē*”
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytuḷḷār. vēṟāy niṉṟu antamum ātiyumām tiṟattai, “vēṟāy īṟāy mutalāy” eṉṟum; oṉṟāy niṉṟu ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷai iyakkum tiṟattai, “oṉṟāy, iru, peṇṇāṇāy, kuṇamūṉṟāy, maṟai nāṉkāy, pūtamavaiyain tāy, āṟār cuvaiyāy, ēḻōcaiyāy, eṭṭut ticai tāṉāy” eṉṟum; uyirkaḷōṭu oṉṟākāmalum vēṟā kāmalum, uṭaṉiruntu kaṇṭum, kāṭṭiyum, utaviyaruḷum, mutalvaṉ tiṟattai “uṭaṉāṉāṉ” eṉṟum ittiruppāṭṭil tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉ aruḷic ceykiṉṟār. ivvāṟu iṟaivaṉatu mūvakai nilaiyiṉaiyum tiru varuḷāḷ uṇarntu oruṅkē eṭuttu ōtiya nūl vaṭamoḻiyilō vēṟu moḻikaḷilō itukāṟum kāṇappaṭavillai. ikkaruttuk kaḷai vaṟpuṟuttuvataṟkākattāṉ. āciriyar, pulavar perumakkaḷāl “antam āti” eṉap pukaḻn tōtappeṟṟa mutalvaṉ, “avaiyē tāṉēyāy niṟkum” eṉṟu iccūttirattiṉ eṭutta eṭuppilēyē iyampi yaruḷukiṉṟār.
ulakil nilavum uyirkaḷ ovvoṉṟum maṉattāl niṉaittalum vāyāṟ collutalum, uṭampāl ceyal ceytalum ceykiṉṟaṉa. ivaiyāvum viṉaiyeṉṟē kūṟappaṭum. ivviṉaikaḷ, iṉpam viḷaiyumāyiṉ nalviṉaiyum, tuṉpam viḷaiyumāyiṉ tīviṉaiyum eṉa iru vakaip peyar peṟum, ivviṉaiyum viṉaippayaṉum uyirpōla aṟivillātaṉa vātalāl, avai tammaic ceyta uyirait tāmē ceṉṟu cērā; viṉaiyaiyum viṉaippayaṉ kaḷaiyum kūṭṭuvippatu mutalvaṉ ceyal. viṉaikkuriya payaṉaiyum, payaṉai uyirkaḷ nukartaṟkuriya uṭal karuvikaḷaiyum mutalvaṉ taṉ āṇaiyākiya ciṟcattiyāl tōṟṟuvippaṉ. avvāṇai, uyirkaḷiṉ viṉai nukarccik kēṟpa uṭal karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟaik  kūṭṭuvatum nīkkuvatum ceyyum. kūṭṭuṅkāl piṟappum, nīkkuṅkāl iṟappu uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. āṉatu paṟṟiyē, uyirkaḷ āṇaiyiṉ iru viṉaiyiṉ pōkkuvaravu puriya eṉṟu āciriyar uraittaruḷu kiṉṟār. pōkku, iṟappu; varavu, piṟappu.
ivviraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷālum, ulakiṟku mutalvaṉ iṟaivaṉ eṉṟum, mutaṟkāraṇam māyai yeṉṟum, tuṇaik kāraṇam āṇaiyākiya aruṭcatti yeṉṟum, ulakap paṭaippukkuk kāraṇam uyirkaḷiṭattiluḷḷa malam eṉṟum, ulaka vāḻviṉkaṇ uyirkaḷ viṉai ceykiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum kaṇṭōm. ivvakaiyāl, iṟaivaṉ, uyir, ulaku eṉṟa mūṉṟum, malam, māyai, viṉai eṉṟa mūṉṟum aṟikiṉṟōm. malam mutaliya mūṉṟaiyum pācam eṉṟum, ivaṟṟōṭu toṭakkuṟum uyiraip pacu eṉṟum, iṟaivaṉaip pati eṉṟum kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. ivaṟṟai uṇarttavē.
“	avaiyē tāṉē yāyiru viṉaiyiṉ
 	pōkku varavu puriya āṇaiyiṉ
 	nīkkam iṉṟi niṟkum aṉṟē”
eṉa aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār.
3. uyireṉpatu oṉṟu uṇṭeṉpatai mūṉṟām cūttirat tāl vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟār. uyirkku āṉmā eṉṟa peyarai vētānta nūlkaḷākiya upaniṭataṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. nam nāṭṭil camaya neṟiyil niṉṟa aṟiñaruḷ cilar āṉmā eṉa oṉṟu kiṭaiyātu eṉṟaṉar; cilar uṭampē āṉmā eṉṟaṉar: cilar intiriyaṅkaḷaiyē āṉmā eṉṟaṉar; kaṉaviṉ kaṇ kāṇappaṭum cūkkuma uṭampaiyē cilar āṉmā eṉṟaṉar; pirāṇavāyuvaiyē āṉmā eṉṟaṉar cilar. ivvāṟu cutta piramamē āṉmā eṉṟum, ellāk karuvikaḷum kūṭiya kūṭṭamē āṉmā eṉṟum cilar kūṟi vantaṉar. avarkaḷai muṟaiyāka eṭuttu maṟuttu avar kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṉ vēṟāka āṉmā eṉṟu oṉṟu uḷatu eṉa āciriyar nilaināṭṭu vārāyiṉar.
uṭampai nōkki, uṭal, poṟi, maṉam mutaliya kūṟukaḷākap pakuttu ovvoṉṟaiyum uyirallaveṉak kaḻittu oṉṟum āṉmā aṉṟu eṉṟu kaṇṭu āṉmā eṉa oṉṟu illaiyeṉpāraip pārttu, ivvāṟu kūṟiṭṭu nōkki āṉmā illaiyeṉa eṇṇik kūṟuvatuyātō atuvē āṉmā eṉṟu collaluṟṟu. “ilatu eṉṟaliṉ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār. uṭalē āṉmā eṉpārai nōkki, “nīṅkaḷ itu eṉatu uṭal eṉkiṟīrkaḷallavā? eṉatu eṉṟa collukkup poruḷāy uḷḷatu yātu?”  eṉṟu kēṭṭu, uṭaliṉ vēṟāka eṉatu eṉṟum coṟkup poru ḷāy iruppatuvē āṉmā; ataṟku uṭaimaiyākiya uṭampu āṉmā aṉṟu eṉṟu teruṭṭutaṟku. “eṉatu uṭal eṉṟaliṉ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār. kaṇ mutaliya intiriyaṅkaḷ poruḷkaḷaip pārttal mutaliya aṟivuc ceyalaic ceyvatāl avvintiriyamē āṉmā eṉpavarkaḷaik kaṇṭu, kaṇ mutaliya poṟikaḷ tattamakkuriya oḷi mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaṇṭaṟiyumēyaṉṟi, kaṇ ōcaiyaiyum kātu oḷiyaiyum ivvāṟē piṟa piṟavaṟṟaiyum aṟiyā; ivaiyē āṉmāvāyiṉ, oṉṟē ellāvaṟṟaiyum aṟiya vēṇṭum. avvāṟu aṟiyāmaiyāl intiriyam āṉmā ākātu eṉpārāy, “aimpulaṉ aṟitaliṉ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār.
iṉi, cūkkuma vuṭampē āṉmā eṉpārai nōkkik kaṉaviṉkaṇ cūkkuma vuṭampu kāṇum kāṭciyai naṉaviṉ kaṇ tūlavuṭampu kaṇṭatākak kūṟuvatāl, ataṟku vēṟāka oṉṟu niṉṟu kāṇkiṟateṉpatu peṟappaṭum. avvoṉṟē āṉmā eṉa aṟika eṉpārāy, “oṭukkam aṟitaliṉ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār. pirāṇavāyuvē āṉmāvāyiṉ, aḵtu naṉavukkālattil nukarum iṉpat tuṉpaṅkaḷai uṟaṅkuṅ kālattilum pirāṇavāyuviṉ iyakkam iruppatāl nukartal vēṇṭum; avvāṟu nukarcci illāmaiyāl pirāṇa vāyu āṉmā aṉṟām. ākavē ataṉiṉ vēṟāka āṉmā uḷatu eṉpār, “kaṇpaṭil uṇṭi viṉai iṉmaiyiṉ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār, uṇṭi yeṉṟatu iṉpat tuṉpaṅkaḷai nukarvata.
iṉi, cutta piramamē āṉmā eṉpavarkaḷaip pārttu, piramam eṉpatu, ellāvaṟṟaiyum oruvar aṟivikka aṟiyāmal oruṅkē oru kuṟaiyumillāmal tāṉē aṟiyum caitaṉṉiyam eṉṟu collukiṟīrkaḷ. āṉmāvō, tāṉē etaiyum aṟiya māṭṭātu, aṟintōr aṟivikka aṟiyum ciṟumaiyuṭaiyatu; ākavē, piramamē āṉmā eṉpatu poruntā teṉpārāy, “uṇartta uṇartaliṉ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār.
iṉi, poṟi mutaliya ellāvaṟṟiṉ kūṭṭamē āṉmā veṉpārkku, poṟi mutaliya ellām oru pāvaiyiṭattum kūṭiyuḷḷaṉa; oru piṇattiṉiṭattum uṭampukkuriya kūṟukaḷ ellām kūṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum āṉmāvukkuriya aṟivu viḻaivu ceyalkaḷ illai. ākavē akkūṭṭattiṉ vēṟāka āṉmā oṉṟu ivvuṭampiṉuḷ uḷatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.poṟi mutaliya karuvikaḷiṉ kūṭṭamākiya uṭampukku mutaṟkāraṇam māyai;ammāyai āṉmā aṉṟu; anta āṉmāvukku inta māyā kāriyamāṉa uṭampu iṭamākum; avvuṭam piṉ ceyal koṇṭu. ataṉuḷ āṉmā iruppatu uṇarappaṭukiṟatu eṉpatai viḷakkutaṟku. “māyā iyantira taṉuviṉuḷ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār. māyā iyantira taṉu - māyaiyiliruntu tōṟṟuvikkap paṭṭa iyantiram pōṉṟa uṭampu. ikkaruttukkaḷaiyē.
“	uḷatuilatu eṉṟaliṉ eṉatuṭal eṉṟaliṉ
 	aimpulaṉ oṭukkam aṟitaliṉ kaṇpaṭil
 	uṇṭiviṉai iṉmaiyiṉ uṇartta uṇartaliṉ
 	māyā iyantira taṉuviṉuḷ āṉmā”
eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.
4. mūṉṟām cūttirattil āciriyar, kāṇappaṭāta uyirai uṇṭu eṉpataṟkākak kāṇappaṭum namatu uṭampaiyē ārāyntu kāṭṭiṉār. nam uṭampil aṟi karuvi, ceyaṟ karuvi eṉa iruvakaik karuvikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. aṟi karuviyai ñāṉēntiriyam eṉṟum, ceyaṟ karuviyaik kaṉmēntiriyam eṉṟum vaṭa moḻiyiṟ kūṟuvar. avaṟṟuḷ ñāṉēntiriyam, meyvāy kaṇ mūkku cevi eṉa aintākum. kaṇmēntiriyam, vāy kāl kai pāyu upattam eṉa aintām. mey mutaliya aṟi karuvikaḷāl ulakiyalai aṟivatākiya ceyalaic ceykiṉṟōm. vāy mutaliya ceyaṟ karuvikaḷāl pēcutal, naṭattal mutaliya ceyalkaḷaic ceykiṉṟōm. ik karuvikaṭku vēṟāka, maṉam cittam akaṅkāram puttiyeṉa nāṉku uṭkaruvikaḷ nam uṭampil uṇṭu. maṉam cittam mutaliyavaṟṟai antakkaraṇam eṉa aṟivu nūlkaḷ kūṟum. uṭampu parupporuḷāyk kaṇṇukkut teriyakkūṭiyatu; uyir kaṇṇukkut teriyāta aruvap poruḷ. uṭampiṉ ellaiyai nīṅkāmal ataṉuḷḷē aṟivāy uṭampiṉ pala vēṟu kūṟukaḷaiyum iyakkum iyakkap poruḷāy uyir irukkiṟatu. uṭampiṉ ellaiyai urumaṇṭalam eṉṟum, aṟivuruvāy nilavum uyirellaiyai aṟivu maṇṭalam eṉṟum aṟiñar kūṟuvar. aṟikaruvikaḷum ceyaṟ karuvikaḷum urumaṇṭalattil iruppavai. uṭkaruvikaḷāṉa antak karaṇaṅkaḷ aṟivu maṇṭalattil irukkiṉṟaṉa. uru maṇṭalattai uruvuṭampu eṉṟum. ataṟkuḷ iruppatu antak karaṇaṅ kaḷait taṉṉiṭattē koṇṭa aruvuṭampu eṉṟum, nammuṭaiya nūlkaḷ kuṟikkum. āṅkilam jermaṉi mutaliya moḻikaḷaip pēcum mēṉāṭṭār lattīṉ moḻiyāl tammuṭaiya ārāycciyiṟ kaṇṭa poruḷ kaḷukkup peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvatu pōla nam nāṭṭār vaṭamoḻiyāl ñāṉēntiriyam, kaṉmēntiriyam, antakkaraṇam eṉap peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvar. ākavē, ivai vaṭacollāl kuṟikkappaṭuvatupaṟṟi vaṭanūlārukkut tāṉ inta ñāṉēntiriya mutaliyaṉavuṇṭu; tamiḻarkaṭku illai yeṉṟu kūṟip piṇaṅkuvatu peruntaṉmai yākātu. ivvāṟē innūlil āṅkāṅkuk kāṇappaṭum vaṭacoṟkaḷ āṭci kāraṇamāka mēṟ koṇṭavaiyeṉak koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, mūṉṟām cūttirattil intiriyaṅkaḷ āṉmā ākā eṉpatu kaṇṭa camayavātikaḷiṟcilar, antakkaraṇamē āṉmā eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. antakkaraṇam āṉmāviṉ ceyalukkuk karuviyākumē tavira āṉmāvākātu eṉpārāy, “antak karaṇam avaṟṟiṉ oṉṟaṉṟu āṉmā  eṉṟu āciriyar nāṉkām cūttirattil kūṟukiṉṟār. kaṇ mutaliya karuvikaḷiṉ vāyilākak kāṇap
paṭuvataip paṟṟuvatu maṉam; ataṉai iṉṉateṉac cintippatu cittam; cintaṉaiyiṉkaṇ itutāṉeṉat tuṇivēṉeṉa eḻuvatu akaṅkāram; tuṇintu taṉkaṇ koḷvatu putti. ivaṟṟaik karuviyākak koṇṭu āṉmā aṟivup poruḷai aṟintu koḷḷum. ivvakaiyil maṉam mutaliya nāṉkum āṉmāvākiya aracaṉukku amaiccar pōla niṉṟu aṟivuttuṇai ceykiṉṟaṉa. ikkaruvikaḷ āṉmāvōṭu kūṭi ataṟku amaiccut toḻil ceyyum eṉpatu viḷaṅkavē, āciriyar, “avai cantittu niṉṟu amaiccaracu ēyppa” eṉṟu kūṟiyaruḷukiṉṟār.
muṉpu kaṇṭa uru maṇṭalamum aṟivu maṇṭalamu mākiya iraṇṭaiyum oṉṟāyaṭakki āṉmatattuvam eṉṟu nam camaya nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉiṉ vēṟāka iruvakait tattuvaṅkaḷ uṇṭu; avai, vittiyā tattuvam civa tattuvam eṉappaṭum. vittiyā tattuvam (Spiritual Sphere) nuṇṇuṭampukku uḷḷāy uyiraic cūḻntu niṟpatu. civatattuvam uyirkku mēlāyt tiruvaruḷ tōytaṟku vāyilāy (Medium) iruppatu. iṟai vaṉuṭaiya aruṭcatti civa tattuvattiṉ vāyilāka āṉmāviṉ aṟivu, viḻaivu, ceyal eṉṟa mūvakaiyāṉa āṟṟalkaḷaiyum kiḷarac ceyyum; civa tattuvattiṉ vāyilāka varum avvaruṭ cattiyē āṉma tattuvattaiyum vittiyā tattuvattaiyum toḻiṟ paṭuttukiṟatu. vittiyā tattuvattāl uyiriṉuṭaiya aṟivu mutaliya āṟṟalkaḷ eḻuntu aṟitaṟkum, viḻaitaṟkum, ceytaṟkum muṟpaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. appōtu āṉma tattuvam aṟital mutaliya ceyalkaḷai nikaḻvikkiṉṟatu. āṉmāvākiya uyiraic cūḻntu āṉmatattuvamum ataṟkuḷ vittiyā tattuvamum, ataṟkuḷḷāka āṉmāvum irukkiṉṟaṉa. itu paṟṟiyē āṉmatattuvattu intiriyaṅkaḷaip puṟakkaraṇam eṉṟum; maṉamutaliyaṉa avaṟṟiṟkuḷḷāka iruppatu paṟṟi akakkaraṇamākiya antakkaraṇam eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṟkum uḷḷēyirukkiṉṟa vittiyā tattuvattai uḷḷantakkaraṇam eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṟkum uḷḷāl irukkiṟa āṉmāvai uḷḷam eṉṟum cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. “ōrttu uḷḷam uḷḷatu uṇariṉ oru talaiyāp. pērttuuḷḷa vēṇṭā piṟappu” eṉṟa (357) tirukkuṟaḷil tiruvaḷḷuvar āṉmāvai uḷḷam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatai iṅkē niṉaivu kūrtal vēṇṭum.
ivvāṉma vittiyā tattuvaṅkaḷukku uḷḷē irukkum āṉmāvukku aṟivu mutaliya mūvakai āṟṟalum iyalpākavē uṇṭeṉṟālum, mutaṟ cūttirattil kāṭṭappaṭṭa malattāl maṟaippuṇṭu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ammalam cempil kaḷimpu pōla iyalpāy āṉmāvil aṉāti toṭarpu koṇṭiruppatāl atu cakacamalam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum iccakacamalattāl maṟaip puṇṭu iruppatu viḷaṅka, āciriyar, “āṉmā cakaca malattu uṇarātu” eṉṟār. cakacam, iyaṟkai. malattāl maṟaippuṇṭu āṟṟal curuṅkiyirukkum āṉmā, avvāṟṟalaic celutti niṉaivaṉa niṉaintu ceyvaṉa ceytu oḻukutaṟku ittattuvaṅkaḷ karuviyāy iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai ōr aracaṉukku amaiccarum piṟa araciyal viṉaiyāḷarum pōla āṉmāvukku amaintuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu paṟṟiyē “amaiccaracu ēyppa” eṉṟār.
iṉi, nammuṭampu, uruvuṭampu (tūlavuṭampu), aruvuṭampu (cūkkuma uṭampu), karuvuṭampu (puriyaṭṭaka vuṭampu), nuṇṇuṭampu (kañcuka vuṭampu), kāraṇa vuṭampu eṉa aintu vakaiyāka ittattuvaṅkaḷaikkoṇṭu pakuttuk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivai nammuṭampiṉuḷ oṉṟiṟkoṉṟu nuṭpamāy amaintuḷḷaṉa. uruvuṭampu toḻiṟpaṭuṅkāl uyir puruva naṭuvil niṟkum eṉṟum, cūkkuma uṭampukkuc celluṅkāl, kaḻuttilum, puriyaṭṭaka vuṭampukkuṭ celluṅkāl itayattilum, kañcuka vuṭampil untiyilum, kāraṇa vuṭampōṭu niṟkaiyil untiyiṉkīḻ mūlātārattilum niṟkum; puru naṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷ uyirukku uṇarvellaiyākum. puruva naṭuvil niṟkum nilai cākkiram (kaṉavu) eṉṟum, kaḻuttil niṟkum nilai coppaṉam (kaṉavu) eṉṟum, itayattil niṟkum nilai cuḻutti (tuyil) eṉṟum, untiyil niṟkum nilai turiyam (uṟakkam) eṉṟum, mūlātārattil niṟkum nilai atītam (pēruṟakkam) eṉṟum nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. puruva naṭuviliruntu mūlātāram varaiyil ittattuvak karuvikaḷai viṭṭup paṭippaṭiyāka nīṅkik kīḻē celluvatum, piṉpu paṭippaṭiyāka mēlēṟip puruva naṭuvukku varuvatum, varumpōtu āṅkāṅku viṭṭa karuvikaḷōṭu kūṭuvatum uyirukku uṭampil uṇṭākum iyakkamākum mēlē kūṟiya aivakai nilaikaḷum avattai eṉappaṭum. itupaṟṟiyē “āṉmā añcavattaittu” eṉṟu kūṟiyaruḷiṉār. itaṉai nāṉkām cūttiram,
“	antak karaṇam avaṟṟiṉ oṉṟaṉṟu avai
 	cantittatu āṉmāc cakacamalat tuṇarātu
	amaiccaracu ēyppa niṉṟu añcavat taittē”
eṉṟu iyampukiṟatu. ivvavattaiyil, kīḻ nōkkic cellum avattai (iyakka nilai) kīḻālavattai yeṉavum, mūlātārat tiliruntu mēlnōkki varum avattai mēlāla vattai yeṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
iccūttirattāl āṉmāvukku aintavattaip paṭuvatu ilakkaṇam eṉpatu peṟappaṭuvatu koṇṭu amaintu viṭalākātu antak karaṇaṅkaḷē aṟital eṉṟa toḻilaic ceyvatāl avaṟṟaiyē uyireṉṟal amaiyum eṉpavar evvāṟu maṟukkappaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉpatai maṟavātu kaṭaip piṭittal vēṇṭum. intak kālattil nilavum mēṉāṭṭu uḷanūlkaḷaik kāṇu miṭattu antakkaraṇamē āṉmāvām eṉṟa kūṟṟu valiyuṟuvatupōlat tōṉṟum. uyir avattaip paṭuvateṉpatu aṟikaruvikaḷōṭu kūṭuvatum kūṭātu nīṅkuvatumākiya nilaiyai eytuvateṉṟu kaṇṭōm. uyir antakkaraṇaṅkaḷōṭu kūṭiya vaḻi aṟivu viḷakka muṇṭākiṟatu; kūṭāvaḻi aṟiviṉatu viḷakkam kuṟaikiṟatu. antak karaṇaṅkaḷē aṟital eṉṉum ceyalai yuṭaiyavāyiṉ, āṉmā aṭaiyum avattai vēṟu pāṭṭuk kēṟpa aṟivu viḷakkattil mikuvatum kuṟaivatum uṇṭātaṟkuk kāraṇamillai. āṉmā kīḻum mēlumāka avattai yeytuṅkāl, niṉṟāṅku niṟkum karuvikaḷ, uyir vantu tōyumpōtu viḷaṅkuvatum nīṅkumpōtu viḷakkam kuṉṟuvatum nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl aṟivākiya oḷiyum ceyalum āṉmāvukkē uriyavāy iruppatu teḷivākiṟatu. ātalāltāṉ āṉmā antak karaṇaṅkaḷākiya avaṟṟiṉ oṉṟaṉṟu eṉpatu vaṟpuṟuttap paṭukiṟatu.
5. antakkaraṇaṅkaḷiṉ vēṟāyc cakacamalattāl uṇarvu maṟaikkappaṭṭu uṭal karuvikaḷōṭu kūṭi niṟkum eṉappaṭṭa āṉmā kīḻum mēlumāka aivakai yavattai eytuvāṉēṉ? avattai eytuṅkāl, karuvikaḷōṭu kūṭi aṟivu viḷakkam peṟuvatum avaṟṟōṭu kūṭāta vaḻi aṟivu oṭuṅkutalum eṟṟukku? eṉṟu cila aiyaṅkaḷ nam niṉaivil eḻukiṉṟaṉa. inta aiyaṅkaḷai eṇṇi ārāyntōr, ōr uṇmaiyaik kaṇṭaṉar; ulakiṭait tōṉṟum poruḷkaḷ aṉaittum iyaṅkiya vaṇṇam irukkiṉṟaṉa. parup poruḷiṉ iyakkam namakkut terikiṟatu; nuṇporuḷiṉ iyakkam pulaṉāvatillai. pulaṉākāmai koṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ kaṇ iyakkam illai yeṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu; ataṉaic cila viññāṉikaḷ nuṇṇiya karuvikaḷāl kāṭṭiyumirukkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl ellāp poruḷiṭattum iyakkam uṇṭu eṉpatu teḷivākiyirukkiṟatu. uyirp poruḷilum ivviyakkam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. enta iyakkamum tāṉē uṇṭākātu eṉpatu potu uṇmai. iyakkamellām kāriyamātalāl, ataṟkuk kāraṇamāy iyakkuvatu oṉṟu uṇṭu. avvāṟē uyirai iyakkuvatum oṉṟu uṇṭu. aḵtu iṟaivaṉatu tiruvaruḷ eṉṟu aṟiñar kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar. ittiruvaruḷ, karuvikaḷōṭu kūṭi avaivēṟu tāṉvēṟu eṉa niṉaiyātu avaiyē tāṉ eṉa mayakkum āṉmāvai, mēlum kīḻum iyakkiyum, karuvikaḷiṟ pirittum kūṭṭiyum uṇmaiyaṟivu peṟa utavukiṟatu.
ivvāṟu taṉakku tōṉṟāt tuṇaiyāy uṭaṉiruntu utavipuriyum mutalvaṉatu aruḷai āṉmā uṇarvatillai; avaṉatu aruḷaiyiṉṟit tāṉ oṉṟum ceytu koḷḷa muṭiyāta ēḻai eṉpataiyum aḵtu eṇṇuvatillai. curuṅkac collu vōmāyiṉ, uyir taṉṉaiyum aṟivatillai; taṉakkut tōṉṟāt tuṇaiyāy uṭaṉiṉṟutavum tiruvaruḷ mutalvaṉaiyum aṟiva tillai. āṉmāvukku mey vāy mutaliya aṟikaruvikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avai tattamakku uriya poruḷkaḷaik kaṇṭu aṟutiyiṭṭu uyirkku uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa; āṉāl avai tammaiyē aṟivatillai; poruḷaik kāṇumāṟum ōcaiyaik kēṭkumāṟum celuttum uyiraiyēṉum akkaṇ mutaliya karuvikaḷ aṟikiṉṟaṉavō eṉiṉ atuvum kiṭaiyātu. atupōlavē uyirkaḷ tammaiyō tamakkut tōṉṟāt tuṇaiyāy niṉṟu utavum tiru varuḷaiyō aṟiyāvā kiṉṟaṉa. itaṉai āciriyar, “viḷampiya vuḷḷattu mey vāy kaṇ mūkku aḷan taṟintu aṟiyā; āṅku avai pōlat tām tam uṇarviṉ tami aruḷ” eṉṟu uraikkiṉṟār. uḷḷam eṉṟatu, āṉma vittiyā tattuvaṅkaṭku uḷḷē aruvāy āṟṟal vaṭivāy irukkum āṉmāvai eṉa aṟika.
tammāl aṟiyappaṭāta mutalvaṉatu tiruvaruḷāl uyirkaḷ aṟivu viḻaivu ceyalvakaikaḷil evvāṟu toḻiṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉiṉ, kāntattiṉ muṉṉē niṟkum irumpu eṅṅaṉam acaikiṟatō aṅṅaṉamē mutalvaṉ tiruvaruḷiṉmuṉ uyirkaḷ aṟivatu viḻaivatu mutaliya ceyalkaḷaic ceykiṉṟaṉa. irumpu acaivatum kāntam acaiyātu iruppatum pōla, tiruvaruḷiṉmuṉ uyirkaḷ aṟivatu mutaliyaṉa ceyya mutalvaṉ oru vikāramum illāmal iruppāṉ. ikkaruttu viḷaṅka āciriyar, “kāntam kaṇṭa pacācat tavaiyē” eṉṟār. pacācam - irumpu. itaṉai,
“	viḷampiya uḷḷattu meyvāy kaṇmūkku
 	aḷantaṟintu aṟiyā āṅkavai pōlat
 	tāntam uṇarviṉ tamiyaruḷ,
 	kāntam kaṇṭa pacācat tavaiyē.”
eṉṟu aintām cūttiram kūṟukiṟatu.
mey vāy kaṇ mūkku cevi eṉṟa karuvikaḷ āṉmāviṉ tuṇaiyāl poruḷkaḷai aṟikiṉṟaṉa; avvāṟu aṟintum, aṟikiṉṟa tammaiyum tammai aṟiyac ceyyum āṉmāvaiyum aṟivatillai; avaṟṟaip pōlavē āṉmākkaḷum tiruvaruḷ tuṇaiyāl ceyvaṉa ceytu, ceyviṉaiyiṉ payaṉai nukar kiṉṟaṉa. avvāṟu nukarntum, nukarkiṉṟa tammaiyum, tamakkut tōṉṟāt tuṇaiyāy uṭaṉiṉṟu iyakkum mutalvaṉ tiruvaruḷaiyum aṟivatillai! tiruvaruḷ iyakka iyaṅkum āṉmāviṉ iyakkam kāntattiṉ muṉpu niṉṟa irumpu iyaṅkuvatu pōlum iyakkamām. appaṭi iyaṅkiṉum caliyāta kāntam pōla, mutalvaṉ calippiṉṟi niruvikāriyāy iruppaṉ eṉpatu itaṉāl uṇarttappaṭum poruḷ.
6. kaṇ kātu mutaliyavaṟṟāl kāṇakkūṭiya parup poruḷ kaḷaiyum ōcai mutaliya nuṇ poruḷ kaḷaiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm. ikkaruvikaḷāl aṟiyap paṭuvaṉa aṉait tum ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷē yākum. ivaṟṟai maṉattiṉkaṇ vaittuc cintippatāl peṟappaṭum uṇarvup poruḷum ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭaṉa vātalāl ulakiyal uṇarvupporuḷkaḷē.
mutaṟ cūttirattiṟ kaṇṭavāṟu ivvulakamē nilai pēṟillātatākum. nilaiyillāta poruḷai acattu eṉṟum, nilaiyuṭaiya poruḷaic cattu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. meyyaic catteṉṟum, poyyai acatteṉṟum kūṟuvatuṇṭu. itu paṟṟiyē “kāṇpa tellām poy” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum makkaḷiṭaiyē nilavukiṟatu. kāṇpateṉpatu, kaṇṇāṟ kāṇpatu, kātāṟ kēṭpatu vāyāṟ cuvaikkappaṭuvatu mutaliyavaṟṟaip potuvākak kuṟikkum. ivvāṟu kaṇ mutaliya aṟikaruvikaḷāl aṟiyappaṭuvaṉa vellām uruvum aruvumākiya ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷēyāy acattāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. evvakaiyālum aṟiyappaṭāta poruḷē kiṭaiyātu. oru poruḷ evvāṟṟālum aṟiyappaṭā teṉṟāl, appaṭi oru poruḷē illai yeṉpatē karuttu. avaṉ, avaḷ, atu eṉap pakuttuk kāṇappaṭum ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷ uruvuṭaiyaṉa vāyiṉum uruvillaṉa vāyiṉum eppaṭiyum avai acattākavē uḷḷaṉa. mutalvaṉākiya paramporuḷ nammuṭaiya aṟivāl aṟiyappaṭuvāṉāyiṉ, aṟiyappaṭum ēṉai ulakiyaṟ poruḷ pōla acattāvāṉ. evvāṟṟālum aṟiyappaṭā toḻiyumāyiṉ, param poruḷ oṉṟu illai yeṉṟē muṭiyum. āciriyar ik karuttaiyē mutalvaṉākiya paramporuḷ, “uṇar uru eṉiṉ acattu; uṇarātu eṉiṉ iṉmai” eṉṟu aruḷiṉār aḵtāvatu iṟaivaṉ nam karuvikaḷāl uṇarappaṭum poruḷ eṉiṉ, acattāvāṉ; uṇarappaṭāṉ eṉiṉ avaṉ eṉṟum evviṭattum illāta poruḷām; aḵtāvatu cūṉiyam eṉpatām.
paramporuḷ oṉṟu uṇṭeṉpatu mutaṟ cūttirattāl tuṇiyappaṭṭatu. ākavē, aḵtu uḷporuḷākiya cattu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. kāṇappaṭum nam uṭampaikkoṇṭu kāṇappaṭāta uyirākiya nām uṇṭeṉpatai mūṉṟām cūttirattāl tuṇin tuḷḷōm. uyirum, paramporuḷ pōla aṉātiyē niṉṟu nilavuvatu; aṟivu viḻaivu ceyal eṉṟa āṟṟalkaḷaiyuṭaiyatu; ataṉāl uyirum cattu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. paramporuḷākiya cattu, uyirākiya cattup poruḷāl oruvakaiyil uṇarappaṭuvatum oruvakaiyil uṇarap paṭāmaiyum uḷḷa cattākiṟatu; oruvāṟṟāl uṇarap paṭuvatāl acattāvatum, uṇarappaṭāmaiyāl cūṉiya māvatum illai. itaṉai āciriyar, “irutiṟa ṉallatu civacattām eṉa iraṇṭu vakaiyiṉ ulaku icaikkum” eṉṟu aruḷukiṉṟār. irutiṟaṉ, acattāvatum cūṉiya māvatumākiya iraṇṭu tiṟaṉ; iraṇṭu vakai, oruvāṟṟāl uṇarap paṭuvatum oruvāṟṟāl uṇarap paṭāmaiyumākiya iruvakai, ulakiyalaṟivum uyiraṟivum koṇṭu uṇarumiṭattu mutalvaṉākiya paramporuḷ uṇarap paṭuvatillai yeṉṟum, apparamporuḷiṉ tiruvaruḷ aṟivu koṇṭu nōkkiṉ apparamporuḷ uṇarappaṭum eṉṟum meyyuṇarvāl paramporuḷaik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟṟa cāṉṟōr kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. “col teriyāp poruḷ cōtikkappāl niṉṟa cōtiyāṉ” eṉṟu tiruñāṉacampantarum, “kāṇpārār kaṇṇutalāyk kāṭṭākkālē” eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracarum kūṟiyaruḷuvatāl mutalvaṉākiya civaparamporuḷ oruvāṟṟāl uṇarappaṭuvatum uṇarappaṭāmaiyum, “aruḷālē meyp poruḷaik kāṇum vitiyuṭaiyēṉ” eṉṟu kāraik kālammaiyārum. “avaṉaruḷē kaṇṇakak kāṇiṉallāl” eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracarum kūṟiyaruḷuvatāl, tiruvaruḷ aṟivu oṉṟē civaparam poruḷaik kāṇṭaṟkut tuṇaiyām eṉpatum caivat tirumuṟaikaḷil vaṟpuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itupaṟṟiyē, “iraṇṭu vakaiyiṉ icaikkumaṉ ulaku” eṉṟār. ulaku eṉpatu tiruvaruḷē kaṇṇākak koṇṭu iṟaivaṉaik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟṟa uyarntōraik kuṟikkum. mēlē kūṟiya cāṉṟōrkaḷai āciriyar, ulaku eṉa uyarttik kūṟiyuḷḷār. 
“	uṇaruru acattu eṉiṉ uṇarātu iṉmaiyiṉ
	irutiṟaṉ allatu civacat tāmeṉa
 	iraṇṭu vakaiyiṉ icaikkumaṉ ulakē.” 
eṉpatu ikkaruttai yuṇarttum cūttiram.
iṅkē kūṟiya ulakiyalaṟivu pācañāṉam eṉṟum, uyiraṟivu pacuñāṉam eṉṟum, tiruvaruḷ aṟivu civañāṉam (patiñāṉam) eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. ulakiyalaṟivu kalvi kēḷvikaḷāl uṇṭākum ceyaṟkaiyaṟivu; itu pācañāṉam, uyiraṟivu uṇmai yaṟiveṉap paṭum iyaṟkaiyaṟivu; itu pacuñāṉam, paramporuḷ pacupāca ñāṉaṅkaḷāl aṟiyap paṭātu, civa ñāṉattāl aṟiyappaṭuvatu paṟṟic civacattu eṉṟu āciriyarāl kuṟikkappaṭukiṟatu.
iccūttirattāl, civa paramporuḷ iyaṟkaiyum ceyaṟkaiyu mākiya nam aṟivellaiyaik kaṭantu niṟpiṉum, ataṉāl aruḷappaṭum civañāṉattāl āṉmākkaḷākiya nām ataṉai aṟital kūṭum eṉpatu vaṟpuṟuttap peṟuvatu kāṇka.
7. iṟaivaṉ uyir ulaku eṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuḷ, iṟaivaṉaic civacattu eṉṟum, ulakai acatteṉṟum muṉṉaic cūttirattāl aṟintōm. cattākiya civam acattai nōkkumāyiṉ, atu nilaiyiṉṟip pōkum marattāl ceyyappaṭṭa yāṉai yoṉṟai atu ceyyavalla oru marattaccaṉ kāṇpāṉāyiṉ, avaṉukkuc cattākiya maram pulaṉākumē aṉṟi accatākiya yāṉai vaṭivam pulappaṭātu. atu pōlavē, cattākiya civattiṉ muṉ acattākiya ulakam nillātu maṟaintoḻiyum, acattākiya ulakam taṉṉi laiyil aṟiyum aṟivu illātatu; ataṉāl atu cattākiya civattai aṟiyātu. itaṉaiyē āciriyar, “cattiṉ etir yāvaiyum cūṉiyam; ākaliṉ, cattu aṟiyātu; acattu ilatu ākaliṉ aṟiyātu” eṉṟār. yāvaiyum eṉṟatu acattākiya ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷaiyākum. avai tōṟṟu vikkattōṉṟi niṉṟu aḻivaṉa vātalāl acattākum. cattākiya civattiṉ muṉ kāriyamākiya avai nillātu pōtu koṇṭu cūṉiyam eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟiyaruḷukiṉṟār. acattu ilatu eṉṟatu acattākiya ulakiyaṟ poruḷ uyirillātatu; ataṉāl aṟivillātatu; ākavē, ataṟku etaṉaiyum aṟiyum tiṟam illai eṉpatām.
ivvakaiyāl, iṟaivaṉ ulaku eṉṟa iraṇṭum cattum acattumāy oṉṟaṉai yoṉṟu aṟiyā eṉpatu kāṇum namakku. ivviraṇṭiṉ vēṟākiya uyir, cattākiya civattaiyum acattākiya ulakattaiyum aṟiyum pōlum eṉṟa aiyamuṇṭākiṟatu. anta aiyattai nīkkavē āciriyar, iraṇṭiṉaiyum aṟiyavallatu āṉmā eṉpārāy, “iru tiṟaṉ aṟivuḷatu iraṇṭalā āṉmā” eṉṟu ēḻām cūttirattāl kūṟukiṉṟār. irutiṟaṉ aṟivuḷatu eṉpatu, cattum acattumākiya iraṇṭiṉaiyum aṟiyum aṟivu uḷḷatu eṉṟum, iraṇṭiṉālum aṟiyum aṟivuḷḷatu eṉṟum, iraṇṭiṉiṭattum niṉṟu aṟiyum aṟivuḷḷatu eṉṟum poruḷ koḷḷappaṭum. cattākiya civattāl aruḷappaṭum civañāṉattāl uyir accivattai aṟiyum aṟivu peṟukiṟatu; acattākiya karuvi karaṇaṅkaḷaik koṇṭē ulakiyalaṟivai aṟiyum aṟivuṭaiyatākiṉṟatu. ataṉāl irutiṟaṉāl aṟiyum aṟivuḷatu eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭatu. civattiṉkaṇ tōyntu civamāy niṉṟu civapōkattai aṟintu nukarvatum, acattākiya ulakiyalil tōyntu ulakapporuḷāy iyaintu ulakiyal iṉpattai aṟintu nukarvatum ceyvatāl, uyirākiya āṉmā iraṇṭiṉpālum aṟiyum aṟivu uḷḷatu eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭatu. ikkaruttellām viḷaṅkavē.
“	yāvaiyum cūṉiyam cattetir, ākaliṉ
 	cattē aṟiyātu acattu ilatu aṟiyātu
	irutiṟaṉ aṟivuḷatu iraṇṭalā āṉmā.”
eṉṟu āciriyar aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār.
itaṉkaṇ cattākiya civattaiyum acattākiya ulakat taiyum vēṟupaṭavaittum, tuṇaiyākak koṇṭum, iṭamāka niṉṟum aṟiyum uyirākiya āṉmā, cattākiya civamum aṉṟu; acattākiya ulakiyaṟ poruḷum aṉṟu eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. itapaṟṟiyē āciriyar, āṉmāvai iraṇṭalā āṉmā eṉṟu kūṟiyaruḷiṉār. cattaṉṟāyiṉum cattōṭiyaintu cattākiya civattaiyum, acattōṭu kūṭi acattāy niṉṟu acattaiyum aṟiyum taṉmaiyuṭaiyatāka iruppatāl inta āṉmāvaic catacattu eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvārāyiṉar. “cattu acattu aṟivatu āṉmā, tāṉ cattum acattum aṉṟu. nittamāyc catacattāki niṉṟiṭum” eṉṟu aruṇanti civaṉārum, “catacattām uyir” eṉṟu umāpati civaṉārum viḷakkiṉār.
8. catacatteṉappaṭum āṉmākkaḷākiya nām acattākiya uṭampōṭu kūṭi acattākiya ulakiyalil aḻunti ulakiyal vāḻkkaiyai naṭattukiṉṟōm. ivvāḻkkaiyil kaṇ,kātu mutaliya karuvikaḷāl aṟivaṉa aṟivatum, kai, kāl, vāy mutaliya karuvikaḷāl  ceyvaṉa ceyvatum nammuṭaiya ceyalkaḷākiṉṟaṉa. icceyalkaḷil īṭupaṭuṅ kāl namakku aṟivu viḷakka muṇṭākiṟatu. appōtu iṉpamum uṇṭākiṟatu. āṉmā anta iṉpattukku aṭimaiyāki, anta acat tākiya karuvikaḷ kāṭṭum kāṭciyaiyē virumpi avai iyaṅkum neṟiyilēyē cellukiṟatu. ulakiyal vāḻkkaiyōṭu toṭarpuṇṭāṉatāl viḷaiyum payaṉkiṭṭātu pōkiṟatu. āṉmā, taṉakkum acattākiya ulakukkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭṭiyalpai yeṇṇi, ulaku uṭal karuvikaḷaik karuviyākak koṇṭu peṟutaṟkuriya payaṉai eytavēṇṭum. appayaṉ uṇmai aṟivāl uṇarappaṭum. anta aṟivaiyum mutalvaṉ uṭaṉāy niṉṟu uṇartti varukiṉṟāṉ. ivvakaiyāl, uṇmai yaṟiviṉ viḷakkattāl, uṭal karuvi ulaka vāḻvukaḷaip payaṉ koṇṭu varum āṉmāvukku, malattāl taṭaiyuṇṭirukkum ciṟumai keṭat toṭaṅkukiṟatu anta nilaiyil uṇṇiṉṟu tōṉṟāt tuṇaiyāy uṇmai aṟivaik kāṭṭiyaruḷum mutalvaṉ, ñāṉāciriyaṉ uruvil āṉmākkaḷiṉ etiril vantu uṇmaiyaṟivākiya civañāṉattai nalkuvaṉ. māṇikkavācakarukkut tirupperuntuṟaiyil kurunta marattiṉkīḻ eḻuntaruḷiyatum, meykaṇṭa tēvarukkut tiruveṇṇeynallūril parañcōti muṉivar eḻuntaruḷiyatum pōla eḻuntaruḷi vantu civañāṉattai utavuvāṉ.
“vēntaṉ oruvaṉuṭai makaṉ vēṭar kūṭṭattiṟ paṭṭu avar taruvaṉavaṟṟaip peṟṟu vaḷarntuvantāṉ. vaḷarntavaṉ, taṉṉai ōr aracaṉ makaṉ eṉpatai maṟantu, vēṭar vayamē niṉṟu taṉṉai avvēṭariṉ vēṟallēṉ eṉṟē niṉaikkaluṟṟāṉ. avaṉ vaḷarcciyaip pārttuk koṇṭē vanta vēntaṉ, avaṉukkuc coṉṉāl kēṭṭut teḷintu accol vaḻi niṟkak kūṭiya cevvi varuvatu kaṇṭāṉ. uṭaṉē tāṉum oru vēṭaṉ uruvil avaṉetiril vantu avaṉōṭu collāṭi, “nī vēṭaṉ allai; vēṭarkaḷiṭaiyē vaḷarntu uṉṉai iṉṉāṉeṉa aṟiyātu maṟantu viṭṭāy; nī oru vēntaṉuṭaiya makaṉ” eṉṟu collit teruṭṭiṉāṉ. uṭaṉē avaṉ, vēntaṉ iyalpukaḷaik kēṭṭaṟintu vēntaṉuṭaiya paṇpukaḷai yeṇṇi vēntaṉāy viḷakkamuṟṟāṉ” eṉpatu pōlum varalāṟṟaic collic civañāṉam uṇarttavarum ñāṉāciriyaṉ, āṉmākkaḷākiya nammait teruṭṭat toṭaṅkuvaṉ. “nāṉ mēlē coṉṉa aracaṉ makaṉ āṉmāvākiya nī; vēṭar kūṭṭam eṉṟatu, uṉakkuk karuvikaḷāka amainta aimpulaṉkaḷākum; avvaracaṉ makaṉ pōlavē āṉmāvākiya nī uṉṉai maṟantu akkaruvikaḷiṉ vayamē niṉṟu oḻukukiṉṟāy; nī ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟu; aṟivē uṉakku uru; uṇmaiyaṟi viṉpamākiya civapōkattai nukartaṟkē  nī uriyai; civāṉanta ñāṉa vaṭivu koṇṭu civa param poruḷiṉ tiruvaṭi nīḻalil eṉṟum poṉṟāta iṉpam peṟutal vēṇṭum” eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttuvaṉ. itaṉai āciriyar, “aimpula vēṭariṉ vaḷarntu ayarn taṉai eṉat tam mutal kuruvumāy uṇartta” eṉṟu aruḷukiṉṟār. tam mutal eṉṟatu āṉmākkaḷukku mutal vaṉākiya iṟaivaṉaik kuṟikkum. kuruvumāy eṉṟatu, itaṟku muṉ ṉellām uyirkkuyirāy uṭaṉiruntu uḷḷē aṟivaruḷi varuvatēyaṉṟi kuruvākavum varukiṉṟāṉ eṉpataik kuṟikkiṟatu. coṉṉa collaik kēṭṭu accolvaḻi niṟkakkūṭiya cevvi varak kaṇṭu vēntaṉ aṟivuṟuttiyatu pōla, āṉ mākkaḷākiya namakku ñāṉavurai kēṭṭu ataṉ vaḻiyē niṟkakkūṭiya takuti varumpōtutāṉ ñāṉāciriyaṉ varuvaṉ. ceyyappaṭum tavattāl tāṉ āṉmākkaḷukku attakutiyum cevviyum uṇṭākum. ātalāl, “tavattiṉil uṇartta” eṉa āciriyar aṟivuṟut taruḷukiṉṟār.
araca kumaraṉukku vēṭar kūṭṭattiṉ toṭarpu, vēntaṉ col kēṭṭu vēntākum paṇpu peṟutaṟkuk karuviyāyiruntu, ataṉaip peṟat talaippaṭṭatum viṭupaṭṭatu pōla, āṉmā ñāṉāciriyaṉatu ñāṉavurai kēṭṭu ñāṉaneṟi yoḻukum cevvi peṟutaṟku uṭal karuvi mutaliya acattiṉ toṭarpu karuviyākiṟatu. ñāṉam peṟṟu ñāṉaneṟi niṟkat talaippaṭuṅkāl āṉmā acattiṉ toṭarpai viṭṭuviṭukiṟatu. “nilaiyāta ulakiyalpu kaṇṭu nilaiyā vāḻkkai yallēṉ”  eṉṟu aṟintu tirunāvukkaracar aṟat tuṇintatu pōla, āṉmā, uṭal, karuvi ulaku mutaliya acattiṉ vēṟātalāl ataṉōṭu uḷatākiya toṭarpu aṟṟoḻukuvatē atu ceyyattakkatu. “oṉṟalāvuyir vāḻkkaiyai niṉaintiṭṭu uṭal taḷarntu arumā niti iyaṟṟi, eṉṟum vāḻalām emakkeṉap pēcam ituvum poy” eṉṟu nampiyārūrar niṉaintu acattiṉ toṭarpai viṭṭatu pōlavum, “eṇṇa mō uṭal, vāy, mūk koṭu, cevi, kaṇ eṉṟivai niṉkaṇē vaittu maṇṇiṉ mēl aṭiyēṉ vāḻkilēṉ” eṉṟu maṇivācakar karuti yoḻukiyatu pōlavum oḻukuvataṉāl acattiṉ toṭarpu viṭum.
āṉmāvukkuc catacattu eṉṟa peyaruṇṭāṉatu, cat tōṭum acattōṭum toṭarpuṟṟu niṟkum iyalpiṉāl tāṉ, cattu acattu eṉṟa iraṇṭiloṉṟaic cārntu niṟpatu uyirkku iyalpēyaṉṟi vēṟē taṉittu niṟkum iyalpu ataṟkuk kiṭaiyātu. catacattākiya āṉmā acattiṉ toṭarpu viṭṭavaḻi, cattākiya civattaiyē ceṉṟu cārum. ūcalāṭum oruvaṉukku ataṉ kayiṟu aṟumāyiṉ avaṉ taraiyaic cārvatu pōla, āṉmā acattiṉ toṭarpuviṭṭāl cattākiya cittaiyē cārum. cattāntaṉ maiyālum cittāntaṉmai (aṟivuṭaiyatu eṉṉum taṉmai) yālum āṉmāvukkuc cattākiya civam anniyamaṉṟu; ataṉāl āṉmā civattaiyē ceṉṟu cārum eṉpatu teḷivām. āṉmāvukku ēṉai uṭal karuvi mutaliya acattu anniyam. acattaic cārntu atuvatu vāy iyaintirunta pōtum, āṉmā acattiṉ vēṟāka iraṇṭu paṭṭu niṉṟatupōliṉṟi cattākiya civattaic cārntu oṉṟukiṟapōtu ataṉkaṇ oṉṟi uṭaṉāy niṟkum. itaṉai uṇarttuvataṟku āciriyar, “viṭṭu aṉṉiyam iṉmaiyiṉ araṉkaḻalcelumē” eṉṟār itaṉai,
“	aimpula vēṭariṉ ayarntaṉai vaḷarnteṉat
 	tammutal kuruvumāyt tavattiṉil uṇarttaviṭṭu
	aṉṉiyam iṉmaiyiṉ araṉkaḻal celumē.”
eṉa varuvatu kāṇka. araṉkaḻal ceṉṟu cērnta tiruñāṉa campantaraic cēkkiḻār perumāṉ, “pōtanilai muṭinta vaḻippukku oṉṟi yuṭaṉāṉār” eṉṟum, tirunāvukkaracarai. “naṇṇariya civāṉanta ñāṉa vaṭivēyāki aṇṇalār cēvaṭik kīḻ āṇṭa aracu amarntiruntār” eṉṟum kūṟuvataṉāl itu naṉku teḷiyappaṭum. “civāṉanta ñāṉa vaṭivēyāki ” eṉṟatu oṉṟutal; “amarntiruntār” eṉṟatu uṭaṉātal. ivvāṟu oṉṟiyuṭaṉātal eṉpataiyē vaṭacollaṟiñar, ananniya mātal eṉṟum attuvitamāyk kalattal eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
9. āṉmāvākiya uyirukku iyaṟkaiyum ceyaṟkaiyumāka iruvakaiyaṟivu uṇṭu. iyaṟkaiyaṟivu eṉpatu, uṭal karuviyāka uyiriṭattuṇṭākum aṉupava aṟivu. ceyaṟkai yaṟiveṉpatu kalvi kēḷvikaḷiṉāl uṇṭākum aṟivu. iyaṟkai yaṟivillāta viṭattuc ceyaṟkaiyaṟivu ettuṇaivariṉum payaṉpaṭātu. “vāyttut taṉakku iyalpāy vanta aṟivu illārkkuc cāttiraṅkaḷ eṉṉa ceyyum” eṉṟu cāṉṟōrkaḷ kūṟuvatu kāṇalām. iyaṟkaiyaṟivu uṭaṟ kūṟṟālum, cūḻnilaiyālum, kuṭivaravālum uḷatāvatu; ceyaṟkaiyaṟivu kaṟkappaṭum kalvi vakaiyālum, kēḷvi vakaiyālum uḷatāvatu. iyaṟkaiyaṉupava vaṟivaip pacuñāṉa meṉṟum, ceyaṟkai yaṟivaip pācañāṉa meṉṟum caiva nūlkaḷ kūṟum. oruvaṉ pacuñāṉattāṟ kaṇṭa kāṭcikaḷaiyē nūl vaṭivil vaittu viṭiṉ, atu taṉṉaip payilum piṟarkkup pācañāṉamāki viṭum. pāca ñāṉattāl peṟuvatu pala aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭuraitta pacu ñāṉat tokutiyē. vētam ākamam mutaliya ellā vaṟṟālum uṇṭākum aṟivu pācañāṉamē yākum. uṭaliḷḷa pala karuvikaḷiṉ vāyilākat tokuvatu pacu ñāṉam; palaruṭaiya pacuñāṉak kāṭcikaḷai oruṅkē tokuttu nalkuvatu pācañāṉam. ākavē, pacupācañāṉaṅkaḷ eṉpavai āṉmākkaḷ tammuṭaiya uṭal karuvi ulakam mutaliyavaṟṟāl peṟum aṟivu eṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭum. ivvuṭal mutaliya karuvikaḷ acattu; acattu eṉavē avaṟṟiṟ keṉat taṉiyē aṟivillai yeṉpatu viḷaṅkum. acattu aṟivila tākaliṉ, cattākiya civattai aṟiyātu eṉṟu āciriyar muṉpē aruḷicceytuḷḷār. ataṉāl, anta acattut tuṇai ceyya vanta pacupācañāṉaṅkaḷ civattai aṟitaṟkut tuṇaiyākā eṉpatu teḷiya viḷaṅku kiṟatu. itu paṟṟiyē āciriyar “ūṉakkaṇ pācam uṇa rāp patiyai” eṉṟār. pācam eṉṟatu pācañāṉam ūṉakkaṇ eṉṟatu. acattākiya kaṇ mutaliya karuvi koṇṭu aṟiya varum pacuñāṉam. kaṇ mutaliya uṭaṟ karuvikaḷ acattākiya ūṉam uṭaiya vātalāl, avaṟṟāl peṟappaṭum ñāṉam. ūṉakkaṇ eṉappaṭṭatu. āṉatu paṟṟiyē, acattākiya uṭampu eṉṟaṟkut tiruñāṉa cam pantar. “ūṉamām uṭampu” eṉṟum, “ūṉattiruḷ” eṉṟum aṟivuṟuttiyuḷḷār. kaṇṇeṉṟatu aṟivu. pacupāca ñāṉaṅkaḷāl civaparamporuḷaip pārkka muṭiyātu. pati eṉṟatu cattākiya civaparam poruḷai eṉa aṟital vēṇṭum.
pacupāca ñāṉaṅkaḷāl kāṇappaṭāta mutalvaṉ avaṉatu tiruvaruḷē kaṇṇākak kāṇpārkkuk kāṭciyaḷippaṉ. ataṉā laṉṟō mutalvaṉukkuc civacattu eṉpatum oru peyarāyiṟṟu. cattātalāl uṇarappaṭāteṉavum, civacattātalāl civañāṉam oṉṟiṉālē uṇarappaṭu meṉavum muṉṉē kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. civañāṉam ñāṉāciriyarāl aruḷappaṭukiṟatu. civañāṉamē kaṇṇākak koṇṭu puṟakkaruvikaḷaik koḷḷāmal akakkaruvi yākiya cintaiyil viḷaṅkum aṟiviṉ kaṇṇē ārāytalē vēṇṭuvatu eṉpatai nām teḷikiṉṟōm. itu teḷiyavē. āciriyar “ūṉakkaṇ pācam uṇarāp patiyai, ñāṉak kaṇṇiṉil cintai nāṭi” eṉṟu aruḷic ceytuḷḷār. ūṉak kaṇṇum pācamum uṇaramuṭiyāta patiyai ñāṉak kaṇ koṇṭu cintaiyiṉkaṇ nāṭuka eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ; aḵtāvatu pacuñāṉattālum, pācañāṉattālum uṇara muṭiyāta iṟaivaṉai avaṉatu tiruvaḷākiya civañāṉattāl cintaiyiṉkaṇ ārāyntu uṇara vēṇṭum eṉpatu ‘kūṟuka’ eṉpataik ‘kūṟi’ eṉṟu cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkuvatu pōla, nāṭuka eṉpatai nāṭi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟār. ikkaruttaiyē aruṇanti civaṉār. “pācañāṉat tālum pacu ñāṉattālum pārppariya paramparaṉaip patiñāṉattālē nēcamoṭum uḷḷattē nāṭi” eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. iṅkē avar  nāṭi eṉpataṟku ārāyka eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷāmal ārāyntu eṉṟē koḷḷukiṉṟār. maṟṟu civañāṉa muṉivarō, ‘nāṭuka’ eṉṟē poruḷ collukiṉṟār. muṉivar uraiyē aṟiñar aṉaivarālum īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu.”
ñāṉāciriyaṉ tiruvaruḷ ñāṉattai nalkap peṟṟāl, āṉmāvākiya nam aṟivukku uṭal karuvi ulakukaḷākiya acattiṉ toṭarpu vēṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟum. ataṉāl akak karuviyākiya cintaiyiṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa ataṉ toṭarpu vantavāṟum, ataṉai nāmākavē mayaṅki avaṉāl piṇip puṇṭu kiṭakku māṟum, namakkum avaṟkum toṭar poṉṟum illātavāṟum viḷaṅkum. avviḷakkattāl āṉmāvākiya nammaiyē attoṭar piṉiṉṟum nīkki niṟuttik kāṇat toṭaṅkuvōm. nammai aṟintālaṉṟi nām nammai attoṭarpiṉiṉṟum nīkka muṭiyātu, nammaiyē aṟiya muyalvōmāyiṉ, aṟiya niṟkiṉṟa uṭal karuvi mutaliya acattai nikkuvatu iṉitu kaikūṭum. ivvāṟē nammuṭaiya muṉṉōr kaḷākiya cāṉṟōrkaḷ muyaṉṟirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. paṭṭiṉat taṭikaḷ.
“	mēviya puṉmayirt tokaiyō, ammayir
 	pāviya tōliṉ parappō, tōliṭaip
 	pukaviṭṭup potinta puṇṇō, puṇṇiṭai
 	ūṟum utirap puṉalō, kūṟuceytu
 	iṭaiyiṭai niṟkum elumpō, elumpiṭai
 	muṭaikeḻu mūḷai viḻutō, vaḻuvaḻuttu
 	uḷḷiṭai yoḻukum vaḻumpō, meḷḷaniṉṟu
 	ūrum puḻuviṉ oḻuṅkō, nīriṭai
 	vaitta malattiṉ kuppaiyō, vaittuk
 	kaṭṭiya narampiṉ kayiṟō, uṭampiṟkuḷ
 	piriyā toṟukkum piṇiyō, teriyātu
 iṉṉatu yāṉeṉṟu aṟiyēṉ, eṉṉai
 eṅkum tēṭiṉaṉ, yātilum kāṇēṉ.........
 tiruviṭai maruta! poruviṭaip pāka!
 maṅkai paṅka! kaṅkai nāyaka! niṉ
 teyvat tiruvaruḷ kaivantu kiṭaittaliṉ
 māyap paṭalam kīṟit tūya
 ñāṉa nāṭṭam peṟṟapiṉ yāṉum
 niṉperun taṉmaiyum kaṇṭēṉ; kāṇṭalum
 eṉṉaiyum kaṇṭēṉ, piṟaraiyum kaṇṭēṉ;
 niṉṉilai yaṉaittaiyum kaṇṭēṉ; eṉṉē!
 niṉṉaik kāṇā māntar
 taṉṉaiyum kāṇāt taṉmaiyōrē ”*
eṉṟum. “nāṉār eṉṉuḷḷamār ñāṉaṅkaḷār eṉṉai yāraṟivār, vāṉōrpirāṉ eṉṉai āṇṭilaṉēl” eṉṟu māṇikkavācakaṉārum ñāṉakkaṇ koṇṭu cintaiyil nāṭiyatu kāṇalām.
ivvaṇṇam, uṭal karuvi mutaliya acattai vēṟu vaittu ārāyumpōtu, kaṭuveyilil pēyttēr (kāṉal) taṇṇīr pōl kāṭci taruvatupōla, nammiṉ vēṟākātu oṉṟāy nāmākavē kāṭciyaḷitta acattup pēyttēr pōla nīṅki maṟaintoḻiyum; oḻiyavē, iṟaivaṉatu tiruvaruḷ nilaiyamākiya avaṉ tiruvaṭi nīḻal kāṭcippaṭum; āṉmāvum anta nīḻalaic ceṉṟu cērntuviṭum. itaṉai āciriyar “urāttuṉait tērtteṉap pācam oruvat taṇṇiḻalām pati” eṉṟu aṟivittaruḷukiṉṟār, paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷum,  “tūya ñāṉa nāṭṭam peṟṟapiṉ māyap paṭalam kīṟi niṉ perun taṉmaiyum kaṇṭēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟuvatu kāṇka. pēyt tērai āciriyar urāttuṉait tēr eṉṟu iyampukiṉṟār. urā eṉpatu ūrā eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam. tuṉai, viraivu, oruvarālum ūrappaṭātu viraintu cellum tēr eṉṟu pēyttēr kuṟikkap paṭukiṟatu. kutirai malaiyai ōṭāk kutirai eṉṟum, vēṅkai marattaip pāyā vēṅkai eṉṟum cāṉṟōr col laḻaku tōṉṟak kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. itaṉai veḷippaṭai yeṉṉum aṇivakai eṉpar. ivvāṟē pēyt tērai, urāttuṉaittēr eṉṟu āciriyar aruḷic ceykiṉṟār. caṅkac cāṉṟōrum, “uruvil pēey ūrāt tēr” (akam. 67) eṉṟu colliyiruk kiṉṟārkaḷ.
ivvāṟu ñāṉanāṭṭam peṟṟu māyappaṭalam kīṟi mutalvaṉ tiruvaṭi nīḻalaikkaṇṭa tirunāvukkaracar,
“	mācil vīṇaiyum mālai matiyamum
 	vīcu teṉṟalum vīṅkiḷa vēṉilum
 	mūcu vaṇṭaṟaip poykaiyum pōṉṟatē
 	īcaṉ entai iṇaiyaṭi nīḻalē.”
eṉṟu tiruvāy malarntaruḷiyirukkiṉṟār.
iṉi, uṭaliṭamāka niṉṟu ñāṉakkaṇ koṇṭu cintaiyil nāṭi, mutalvaṉ tiruvaruḷ nilaiyaik kaṇṭa piṉpu, ataṉiṭamē oṉṟiyiruppataṟkē uḷḷattil viruppa muṇṭākum; tiruvaruḷ kāṭci tōṉṟum pōtē pēriṉpam curakkum; “cintipvarkkuc ciṟantu centēṉ muntip poḻivaṉa (mutalvaṉ) aṭittalam” eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracar aruḷuvataik kāṇalām. iṉpattai nāṭuvatē uyirkku iyalpātalāl, nam uḷḷam tiruvaruḷ kāṭciyiṉpattaip peṟuvataṟkē peritum virumpum. āyiṉum, avviruppattai, uṭal karuvi ulakukaḷ pōntu citaittu, viruppamainta uyiriṉ uḷḷattait tām kavarntu koḷḷum. pāci paṭarnta nīrnilaiyil kallaiviṭṭeṟintāl, kal viḻuntaviṭattil appāci ciṟitu nīṅkum; piṉṉar vantu mūṭikkoḷḷum; atuvē pōl, tiruvaruṭkāṭciyil muyalumpōtu acattākiya pācattil toṭarpu nīṅkum akkāṭci nīṅkumpōtu ap pācam vantu mūṭiviṭukiṟatu. itaṉai,  “pācipaṭu kuṭṭattil kalliṉai viṭṭeṟiyappaṭum poḻu tu nīṅki atu viṭumpoḻutil parakkum; mācu paṭumalamāyai aruṅkaṉmam aṉaittum araṉaṭiyai uṇarum pōtu akalum, piṉ naṇukum” eṉṟu aruṇanti civaṉār nam āciriyar pāl nērmukamākak kēṭṭaṟintu uṇarntu collu kiṉṟār araṉaṭiyai uṇarum uyir, anta aṭiyiṉ kīḻē irukka virumpum eṉpatu uṇmaiyē; āṉāl avvāṟu iruppataṟku vaḻi yātu? ataṟku oru vaḻi uṇṭu; aḵtāvatu tiruvain teḻuttai iṭaiyaṟāmal muṟaippaṭi eṇṇuvatu eṉpārāy, “viti eṇṇum añceḻuttē” eṉṟu āciriyar aṟivuṟuttaruḷu
kiṉṟār. eṇṇum - eṇṇa vēṇṭum; nīr itaic collum eṉṟāl nīr itaic colla vēṇṭum eṉṟu poruḷāvatupōla eṇṇum eṉpatu eṇṇa vēṇṭum eṉṟu poruḷpaṭukiṟatu.
iṉi, tiruvain teḻutteṉpatu, ci, va, ya, na, ma eṉṟa ainteḻuttukkaḷaiyum kuṟikkum. ivvainteḻuttaiyum kūṭṭavarum civaya nama eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil oru coṟ ṟoṭarākum. iṅkē, “atu coṟṟoṭar aṉṟu; eḻuttu,” eṉpataṟkākavē tiruvainteḻuttu eṉṟu ciṟappāka eṭuttuk kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivveḻuttukkaḷ iṟaivaṉ tiruppeyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa namaciva eṉpatil uḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷ. namaci vāya eṉpatu iṟaivaṉ tiruppeyar. atai māṟṟic civāyanama eṉṟāl peyarttaṉmai nīṅki eḻuttākiviṭukiṟatu tiruvain teḻuttai niṉaintu ōtavēṇṭumāyiṉ, namacivāya eṉṟa tiruppeyarai māṟṟi eḻuttākkuvatu mutal viti; illaiyāṉāl tiruvain teḻuttukkaḷai ilakkaṇappaṭi peṟa muṭiyātu. “nanti nāmam namaccivāya” eṉṟu tiruñāṉa campantarum, “entaiyār tirunāmam namaccivāya” eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracarum aruḷic ceytiruppatu kāṇka.
ivveḻuttukkaḷil ci eṉpatu civaparamporuḷaiyum, va eṉpatu avaṉatu aruḷaiyum, ya eṉpatu uyiraiyum, na eṉpatu uyir acattōṭu kūṭum pōtu cattai niṉaiyātu acattāy iyaintu acattu nalkum nukarccikaḷai nukarvataṟkum, acattai viṭṭuc cattākiya civattōṭu kūṭumpōtu acattai niṉaiyātu civattōṭu civamāy iyaintu civapōkat tai nukarvataṟkum uyirai maṟaittu niṉṟu iyakkum iṟaivaṉu ṭaiya maṟaippuc cattiyaiyum, ma eṉpatu malattaiyum kuṟik kum. ivveḻut taintiṉaiyum eṇṇuṅkāl, civattukkum uyirkaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpum, civattukkum ulakattukku muḷḷa toṭarpum, uyirkaḷukkum ulakattukkumuḷḷa toṭarpum, attoṭar puṇṭāṉa kāraṇamum, ataṉāl viḷaiyum payaṉum eṇṇattukku vantu iṟaivaṉ tiruvaruḷ nīḻalai viṭṭu nīṅkātu niṟkum nilaimaiyaik kaivarac ceyyum. itaṉai,
“	aṇṇal mutalā aḻakār eḻuttaintum
 	eṇṇil irāppakalaṟ ṟiṉpattē-naṇṇi
 	aruḷā ṉatucivattē yākkum aṇuvai
 	iruḷā ṉatutīra iṉṟu*”
eṉṟu tiruvatikai-maṉavācaṅ kaṭantār kūṟiyaruḷuvatu kāṇalām. ivvāṟu ittiruvainteḻuttaiyē puṇaiyākap paṟṟik koṇṭu iṭaiyaṟātu eṇṇip pēriṉpam curakkap peṟṟu nukarnta maṇivācakap perumāṉ.
“	nāṉēyō tavam ceytēṉ! civayanama eṉappeṟṟēṉ.
 	tēṉāy iṉ ṉamutamumā māyt tittikkum civaperumāṉ
 	tāṉēvantu eṉatuḷḷam pukuntuaṭiyēṟ karuḷceytāṉ
 	ūṉāru muyirvāḻkkai yoṟuttaṉṟē veṟuttiṭavē.”
eṉṟu tēṉcoṭṭap pāṭum tiruvācakattāl nām aṟiya aruḷic ceykiṉṟār.
10. ivvāṟu tiruvainteḻuttai vitippaṭi yeṇṇu vatāl iṟaivaṉukkum ulakukkum ulakil vāḻum uyirkaḷākiya namakkum uḷḷa toṭarpu viḷaṅkum eṉṟu muṉpu kaṇṭōm. nuṇṇiya māyaiyākiya uḷporuḷiliruntu uṭal karuvi ulaku mutaliyavaṟṟai iṟaivaṉ paṭaikkiṟāṉ. ataṉāl avaṉ ceypavaṉākiṉṟāṉ; uṭal karuviyulakukaḷ ceyyappaṭum poruḷkaḷākiṉṟaṉa. ceyyappaṭum poruḷ ceypavaṉatu uṭaimaiyākum. evvāṟeṉiṉ, māyai eṉpatu mutalvaṉuṭaiya tiruvaruḷāṟṟalai (cattiyai) iṭamākak koṇṭu kiṭakkum oruvakai āṟṟal; anta āṟṟalaikkalakki uṭal karuvi ulakukaḷaic ceytaruḷuvatu paṟṟi avaṉ ceypavaṉāṉāṉ; avai ceyappaṭu poruḷkaḷāyiṉa. taṉpāl uḷḷa oṉṟaikkoṇṭu oru cey poruḷai oruvaṉ ceyvāṉāyiṉ acceyappaṭuporuḷ avaṉatu uṭaimaiyākiṟa taṉṟō? atupōlavē ivvulakamum ulakiyaṟ poruḷum avaṟṟiṭai nilavum nam uṭalum ataṟkuḷ irukkum karuvikaḷum ākiyayāvum iṟaivaṉuṭaiya uṭaimai yākum. itupaṟṟiyē maṇivācakar mutaliya aruṭ celvar iṟaivaṉai, “uṭaiyavaṉē” eṉṟum, “uṭaiyāy” eṉṟum aṭikkaṭi eṭuttōtiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivvuṭal karuvi mutaliyaṉa iṟaivaṉatu uṭaimaiyēyāyiṉum ivaṟṟaip payaṉkoḷḷuvatu uyirākiya āṉmāvēyaṉṟi avaṉaṉṟu. āṉmā ivvuṭal karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟait taṉṉuṭaiya poruḷākavē eṇṇi avaṟṟiṉ pāl paṟṟukkoḷḷukiṟatu. tantai oruvaṉ oru caṭṭaiyait taittuk koṇṭuvantu taṉ makaṉukkuk koṭuttāl, avaṉ ataṉait taṉatākak koṇṭu ataṉpāl paṟṟukkoḷḷukiṟāṉ; atupōlavē, āṉmāviṉ ceykaiyum uḷatu. tāṉ paṟṟu vaittup payaṉ koḷḷum caṭṭaiyai utaviya tantaiyai, makaṉ “entaiyē” eṉa aṉpu ceyyum muṟaimaiyilēyē, nam cāṉṟōr civaperumāṉāṉa mutalvaṉait tantaiyē eṉṟum, entaiyē eṉṟum, palapaṭiyāka aṉpu moḻikaḷāl aḻaippatai avarkaḷiṉ tirumuṟaikaḷil pārkkiṉṟōm.
iṉi, ‘iṟaivaṉ ivaṟṟai uṇṭupaṇṇi āṉmākkaḷukkuk koṭuppāṉēṉ’ eṉṟōr eṇṇam innilaiyil namakku uṇṭākiṟatu. āṉmākkaḷai anātiyē malamāṉatu piṇittu maṟaittu iruḷil kiṭattiyirukkiṟatu; taṉṉaip pōlavē aṟivu viḻaivu ceyal mutaliya āṟṟal paṭaitta cattākiya āṉmā malattāl kaṭṭuṇṭirukkum nilaiyai nōkkiyaruḷum iṟaivaṉ, āṉmāviṉpāl pēraruḷ koṇṭu māyaiyiliruntu ivvuṭal karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṭaittuk koṭuttu utavukiṉṟāṉ. āṟṟal vaṭivākiya māyai eṉpatu oḷi mayamāka irukkiṟateṉṟu iṉṟaiya viññāṉa aṟivu uṟutippaṭuttukitu. malaviruḷil kiṭakkum uyir āṟṟalākiya oḷipporuḷiliruntu uṇṭākkappaṭum uṭal karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟait tuṇaiyākak koṇṭu, aṟivatum, viḻaivatum, ceyvatum ceytu malaviruḷiliruntu viṭutalaipeṟa muyalkiṟatu.  ivvakaiyil mutalvaṉ taṉporuṭṭu uṭal karuvi mutaliya vaṟṟaip paṭaittu malaviruḷiliruntu vīṭupēṟu eytuvataṟ kākat taṉakku nalkiyiruppatai uyir niṉaikkum; niṉainta vaḻi, ataṟku am mutalvaṉpāl aḷaviṟanta naṉṟiyuṇarvu uṇṭākum. ataṉāl ovvoru kaṇamum iṟaivaṉukku naṉṟi celuttum kaṭamai uyirkku mikukiṉṟatu. 
ivvaṇṇam uṭal karuvikaḷai nalkiya iṟaivaṉ, am maṭṭil niṉṟu pōkātu, avaṟṟōṭu kalantu oṉṟāyum, uyiroṭu kūṭi uṭaṉāyum uṇṇiṉṟu tōṉṟāttuṇai ceyvatu tiruvain teḻuttai vitip paṭi ōtuvatāl uṇarappaṭukiṟatu; uṭalōṭu kūṭi uyir ceyyum ovvoru ceyal nikaḻccikkum iṟaivaṉ tōṉṟā mutalāy iruppatum viḷaṅkukiṟatu. avaṉatu kalappillaiyāyiṉ uṭal karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟil oḻuṅkāṉa ceyal nikaḻātu; malattāl maṟaip puṇṭiruttalāl uyirkkum aṟivu mutaliyaṉa toḻiṟ paṭā; ‘avaṉaṉṟi ōraṇuvum acaiyātu’ eṉpatu uṇmaiyākiṟatu.
ivvāṟu ellāc ceyaṟkum iṟaivaṉē muḻumutalāka iruppavum, avaṉ uyirai muṉṉiruttit tāṉ ataṉōṭu oṉṟiyuṭaṉāy niṟkiṉṟāṉ. ataṉāl, uyir, etaiyum tāṉē ceyvatākavum tāṉē muḻu mutalākavum niṉaintum colliyum ceytum oḻukukiṟatu. itaṉāl viḷaiyum naṉmai tīmaikaṭku uyirē poṟuppāḷiyākiṉṟatu. appoṟuppukku āṉmākkaḷākiya nām uriyarākā viṭṭāl viḷaiyum naṉmai tīmaikaḷ  nammaic cēra māṭṭā. itaṟku vaḻi yātu?
